Access aws:
    1) management console(protected by password + MFA)
    2) cli (protected by access keys)
    3) sdk (for code, protected by access keys)
    Access key ID ~= username
    Secret Access Key ~= password
IAM:
    IAM Security Tools:
        iam credential report (account-level):
            a report of lists all acc users and status of their credentials
        iam Last Accessed in iam user detail (old name: access advisor)(user-level):
            service permissions granted to a user. and service who last accessed.
    IAM Guidelins Best Practices:
        one physical user = one aws user
        assign user to group, and assign permissions to group.
        use and enforce MFA.
        create and use Roles for giving permissions to AWS services.
        Audit permissions of your account using credential report & Last accessed.
        ** Never share iam users & access keys **
    Summary:
        Users: physical users, has a password for console.
        Groups: contains users only.
        Policies: json doc that outlines permissions for users to groups.
        Roles: EC2 instances or aws services.
        Security: MFA + password Policy.
        cli: manage services using command line.
        sdk: manage services using programming language.
    Questions:
        1)IAM Users don't have to belong to a User Group. => 
            IAM User တစ်ယောက် create လုပ်ရင် group ထဲမထည့်လည်းရတယ်
            Grammar:
                don't have to => မလုပ်လည်းရတယ်, မလိုအပ်ဘူး
                Not "မလုပ်ရဘူး"
        2) IAM User Groups can contain IAM Users and other User Groups.
            Ans is false because group can contain only users. not do hierarchy of groups.
        3) An IAM policy consists of one or more statements. A statement in an IAM Policy consists of the following, 
        EXCEPT:
            effect,
            Principal
            Version
            Action
            Resource
        Ans: Version. because Version is part of policy, not the statement.
        Statement have Sid, Effect, Principal, Action, Resource and Condition.
EC2:
    Security Groups:
        as a "firewall" on ec2 instances.
        control how traffic is allowed into or out of ec2.
        only contain allow rules,
        can reference by IP or by Security group.
        Security Groups are VPC scoped.(Security Groups are locked down to a Region/VPC combination)
            Security Group တစ်ခုဟာ
            Region တစ်ခု + VPC တစ်ခု အတွင်းမှာပဲ အသုံးပြုနိုင်တယ်။
                eg:
                    သင်မှာ:
                        Region: Tokyo (ap-northeast-1)
                        VPC: Production-VPC
                        ရှိတယ်ဆိုပါစို့။
                        Security Group:
                        WebServer-SG
                        ကို create လုပ်လိုက်တယ်။
                        ဒီ Security Group ကို:
                        ✅ Tokyo Region ထဲက
                        ✅ Production-VPC ထဲက
                        EC2 တွေမှာပဲ attach လုပ်နိုင်တယ်။
        Does live "outside" the EC2 - if traffic is blocked the EC2 instance won't see it.
            Security Group က EC2 instance အတွင်းမှာ မဟုတ်ဘဲ
            EC2 အပြင်ဘက်မှာ firewall အနေနဲ့ အလုပ်လုပ်တယ်။

            Traffic တစ်ခုကို Security Group က block လုပ်လိုက်ရင်
            EC2 instance ထိတောင် မရောက်ဘူး။
                e.g:
                EC2 တစ်လုံးရှိတယ်:

                EC2 Web Server
                IP: 10.0.1.10

                Security Group Rule:

                Allow:
                HTTP (80) from anywhere

                NO SSH (22)

                Hacker က SSH နဲ့ connect လုပ်ဖို့ ကြိုးစားတယ်:

                1. Internet
                    ↓
                2. Security Group
                    ↓
                3. EC2

                Security Group က စစ်ဆေးတယ်။

                SSH (22) ?
                Not Allowed

                ဆိုတော့:

                ❌ Drop

                EC2 က ဘာမြင်လဲ?

                Nothing

                EC2 ထိ request မရောက်ဘူး။
                Log ထဲတောင် မရှိနိုင်ဘူး။
                Security Group က:
                instance level firewall

                လို့ ခေါ်ပေမယ့်

                တကယ်တမ်း:

                EC2 Operating System ထဲမှာ run နေတာ မဟုတ်ဘူး

                AWS networking layer မှာ run နေတာ။
            Compare With Linux Firewall
                Linux Server ထဲမှာ:

                iptables
                ufw
                firewalld

                သုံးရင်

                traffic က server ထဲရောက်ပြီးမှ
                OS firewall က block လုပ်တယ်

                Security Group ဆိုရင်:

                traffic က EC2 မရောက်ခင်
                AWS က block လုပ်တယ်
            ဒါကြောင့်:
                ✅ Security Group = AWS network layer
                ❌ Security Group = EC2 OS firewall မဟုတ်
                Security Group = EC2 ရဲ့ အပြင်ဘက် gate keeper
                မဝင်ခွင့်ပေးရင်
                EC2 က request ရှိခဲ့တာတောင် မသိဘူး။
        It's good to maintain one separate security group for SSH acccess.
            SSH access အတွက် သီးသန့် Security Group တစ်ခုထားတာ ကောင်းတဲ့ practice ဖြစ်တယ်။
            Security Group တစ်ခုထဲမှာ:
                HTTP 80
                HTTPS 443
                SSH 22
                အားလုံးပေါင်းထည့်ထားတယ်။
                အလုပ်တော့လုပ်တယ်။
                ဒါပေမယ့် manage လုပ်ရခက်လာနိုင်တယ်။
                Better Practice
                    Security Group ခွဲထားမယ်။

                    Web Traffic SG
                    Web-SG

                    Allow:
                    80
                    443
                    SSH SG
                    SSH-SG

                    Allow:
                    22
                    Only from:
                    Your Office IP
                    Your Home IP
        How can administrators securely access EC2 instances in a private subnet?
        ans: Bastion Host.
            Bastion Host =  SSH ဝင်ဖို့အတွက် သီးသန့်ထားတဲ့ EC2 server
            Without Bastion
                Internet
                ↓
                Web1 (SSH open)
                App1 (SSH open)
                DB1 (SSH open)
                Server အားလုံး Port 22 ဖွင့်ထားရမယ်။
                ❌ Security မကောင်း
            With Bastion Host
                Internet
                ↓
                Bastion Host (Public Subnet)
                ↓ SSH
                Private EC2 Servers
            Admin က:
            Bastion Host ကို SSH ဝင်မယ်
            Bastion Host ကနေ Private EC2 တွေကို SSH ထပ်ဝင်မယ်
        regulate:
            access to Ports,
            authorised IP ranges
        If Connection timed out
            Request timed out
            Timeout
            =
            Network/Security Problem
            (Security Group, NACL, Routing)
        If Connection Refused
            =
            Application Problem
            (App not running, wrong port)
        Classic Ports:
            22 = SSH(Secure shell),
            21 = FTP(File Transfer Protocol),
            22 = SFTP(Secure FIle Transfer Protocol)
            80 = HTTP(access unsecured websites)
            443 = HTTPS(access secured websites)
            3389 = RDP (Remote desktop Protocol) Login into a window instance.
        User Data:
            #! /bin/bash
            # Use this for your user data (script from top to bottom)
            # install httpd (linux 2 version)
            yum update -y
            yum install -y httpd
            systemctl start httpd
            systemctl enable httpd
            echo "<h1>Hello World from $(hostname -f)</h1>" > /var/www/html/index.html
        SSH Summary:
            Mac, Linux, Windows >= 10 can do with ssh.
            for only Windows < 10, Putty should use.
            for all, EC2 instance Connect can do.
            for linux/mac osx,
                cmd: ssh -i kms_key.pem ec2-user@public ip
                to readonly file: chmod 0400 kms_key.pem
                to edit file: use sudo
                to check current user: whoami
                to check current directory: pwd
                to check internet access: ping google.com
            for window > 10, all same flow of mac osx.
                if reatonly action need, change file -> properties -> Security -> Advanced -> Disable inheritance -> Remove all... -> 
                    Add -> owner user -> Apply -> OK -> 
            for window < 10,
                download: https://putty.org/index.html
                Before Putty, we need to create ppk file from existing pem file.
                first run PuTTYgen, Load -> kms_key.pem -> Save private key -> Yes -> file name set: name.ppk
                Run Putty, Host Name -> ec2-user@public ip -> ssh -> Saved Sessions -> Save -> Accept -> login as: ec2-user, 
                Connection's SSH's Auth -> choose ppk key.
            EC2 Instance Connect:
                run via ssh. So, need to alow SSH port 22 on security group.
        IAM Role:
            Never Don't add aws account with aws configure within instance.
            Use Instance -> Security -> IAM Role.
    Purchasing Options:
        On-Demand Instances
            short workload, predictable pricing. pay by second.
        Reserved
            1 ~ 3 yrs
            Reserved instances: long workloads
            Convertible reserved instances: long workloads with flexible instances.
        Saving Plans
            1 ~ 3 yrs.
            long workload.
        Spot Instances
            short workloads, cheap, can lose instances(less reliable)
        Dedicated Hosts:
            book an entire physical server.
        Dedicated Instances:
            no other customers will share your hardware.
        Capacity Reservations:
            reserve capacity in specific AZ for any duration. 
        More Note:
            On-Demand:
                Linux Or Window - per second, after first min.
                all other OS - per hour.
                has highest cost but no upfront payment.
                no long term.
                Recommended for short-term and un-interrupted workloads.
            Reserved:
                72% discount compared to On-demand.
                can reserve specific attributes(instance type, region, tenancy, os)
                payment options: noupfront, partial upfront, all upfront.
                scope: regional or zonal
                Recommended for steady-state applications(think database)
                can buy and sell in reserved instance marketplace.
            Savign Plans:
                get discount based on long-term (same as RIs)
                locked to a specific instance family, region.
            Spot:
                discount up to 90%.
                most cost-efficient instances.
                useful for workloads
                    batch jobs
                    data analysic
                    image processing
                    any distructed workloads
                Spot Instance Request:
                    Define max spot price and get the instance while current spot price < max
                    the hourly spot price vaires based on offer.
                    if current spot price > your max price you can choose to stop or terminate your instance with a 2 min grace period.
                        explanation:
                            What is a Spot Instance Request?
                            Spot Instance ဆိုတာ
                            AWS မှာ မသုံးနေတဲ့ EC2 server ကို
                            ဈေးသက်သက်သာသာနဲ့ ငှားသုံးတာ။

                            ဒါပေမယ့်

                            AWS က server ပြန်လိုလာရင်

                            "2 မိနစ်အတွင်း
                            ပြန်ပေးပါ"

                            လို့ ပြောနိုင်တယ်။

                            သင်က

                            အများဆုံး(max spot price)
                            $0.30/hour
                            ပဲပေးမယ်

                            လို့ သတ်မှတ်ထားတယ်ဆိုပါစို့။

                            Spot price က

                            $0.20

                            ဆိုရင်

                            ✅ EC2 ရမယ်။

                            ဒါပေမယ့်

                            Spot price က

                            $0.40

                            ဖြစ်သွားရင်

                            AWS က

                            2 မိနစ်ပေးပြီး
                            instance ကို
                            Stop သို့မဟုတ် Terminate
                            လုပ်နိုင်တယ်။
                            During those 2 minutes you can:
                                Save your work
                                Upload files to S3
                                Finish current tasks
                                Shut down gracefully
                            Because AWS suddenly needs those physical servers for:
                                On-Demand customers
                                Capacity Reservations
                                Higher-priority workloads
                            AWS must reclaim the capacity.
                    How to terminate Spot Instances? you can only cancel spot instance requests that are open, active, disabled.
                        explanation:
                            One-Time vs Persistent Request

                            This is the most important part.

                            There are two request types.

                            ① One-Time Request

                            Imagine you tell AWS:

                            I need one Spot Instance.

                            AWS finds one.

                            Spot Request
                                │
                                ▼
                            Launch EC2

                            Later, the instance is interrupted or terminated.

                            What happens?

                            Nothing.

                            The request is finished.

                            AWS does NOT launch another EC2.

                            Example:

                            Request
                                │
                            Launch EC2
                                │
                            Interrupted
                                │
                            Finished
                            Myanmar

                            One-Time ဆိုတာ

                            တစ်ခါပဲ တောင်းတာ

                            Instance ပျက်သွားရင်

                            AWS က အသစ်ပြန်မဖန်တီးဘူး။
                            ② Persistent Request

                            Now imagine you tell AWS:

                            I always want one Spot Instance running.

                            AWS launches one.

                            Spot Request
                                │
                                ▼
                            EC2 Running

                            If AWS interrupts it:

                            EC2 Interrupted
                                │
                                ▼
                            AWS automatically tries
                            to launch another one

                            As soon as capacity becomes available, AWS starts a replacement.

                            Myanmar

                            Persistent ဆိုတာ

                            အမြဲတမ်း EC2 ရှိနေချင်တယ်။

                            တစ်လုံးပျက်သွားရင်

                            AWS က အသစ်တစ်လုံး
                            ထပ်ရှာပြီး run ပေးမယ်။

                            Step 3: Why Can't I Just Terminate the EC2?

                            Suppose you have:

                            Persistent Request
                                    │
                                    ▼
                            Spot EC2

                            You terminate the EC2 manually.

                            What happens?

                            AWS sees:

                            The request still exists!

                            So AWS launches another EC2.

                            Terminate EC2
                                │
                                ▼
                            AWS creates new EC2

                            Many beginners are surprised by this behavior.

                            To Stop Everything

                            You must:

                            Cancel the Spot Request
                            Then terminate the Spot Instance (if it's still running)

                            Otherwise AWS continues trying to satisfy the request.

                            Step 4: What Does This Mean?

                            Your note says:

                            You can only cancel spot instance requests that are open, active, disabled.

                            These are the request states.

                            Open

                            AWS is still looking for capacity.

                            Need EC2
                            Searching...

                            No instance yet.

                            Active

                            AWS found capacity.

                            Spot Request
                                │
                                ▼
                            EC2 Running
                            Disabled

                            The request exists, but AWS isn't launching instances right now.

                            Think of it as paused.

                            Only these request states can be cancelled.

                            If a request is already completed or cancelled, there's nothing left to cancel.

                            Real Example

                            Suppose you create:

                            Persistent Request

                            1 x t3.medium

                            AWS launches:

                            EC2 #1

                            AWS later interrupts it.

                            EC2 #1
                            Stopped

                            AWS automatically launches:

                            EC2 #2

                            You don't want any more Spot Instances.

                            Wrong way:

                            Terminate EC2 #2

                            AWS immediately launches:

                            EC2 #3

                            Correct way:

                            Cancel Persistent Request

                            Then
                            Terminate EC2
                            Now AWS stops creating new instances.
                    Spot Fleets:
                        Fleet means A group of EC2 instances.
                        Spot Instance အများကြီးကို
                        တစ်ခါတည်း စီမံခန့်ခွဲတဲ့ service ဖြစ်တယ်။
                        AWS ကို
                        m5.large မရရင်
                        c5.large သုံး
                        ဒါလည်းမရရင်
                        m6i.large သုံး
                        AZ-a မရရင်
                        AZ-b သုံး
                        လို့ ပြောထားနိုင်တယ်။
                        AWS က
                        ရနိုင်တဲ့နေရာကနေ
                        အလိုအလျောက်ရွေးပြီး
                        100 လုံးပြည့်အောင် ကြိုးစားပေးတယ်။
                        eg:
                        Need 20 EC2
                                │
                                ▼
                        Spot Fleet
                                │
                        ┌──────┼──────┐
                        │      │      │
                        ▼      ▼      ▼
                        Pool1  Pool2  Pool3
                        │      │      │
                        5      8      7
                                │
                                ▼
                        20 EC2 ✓
                        You can tell AWS:
                            Don't spend
                            more than
                            $0.25/hour
                            Imagine you need:
                            100 taxis

                            Option 1:

                            Only use Toyota Prius.

                            If only 50 are available:

                            ❌ You get 50 taxis.

                            Option 2:

                            Accept:

                            Toyota
                            Honda
                            Nissan
                            Mazda

                            Now it's much easier to find 100 taxis.

                            Spot Fleet works the same way.
                            Example
                                Need:
                                10 servers
                                Configuration:
                                8 Spot
                                2 On-Demand
                            Spot Instance = one cheap server
                            Spot Fleet = many cheap servers, intelligently managed
                            | Spot Instance                     | Spot Fleet              |
                            | --------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
                            | One request                       | Many requests           |
                            | One instance type                 | Multiple instance types |
                            | One Availability Zone (typically) | Multiple AZs            |
                            | Easier                            | Smarter                 |
                            | Less available                    | Higher availability     |
                        Strategies to allocate Spot Instances:
                            lowestPrice
                                ဈေးအသက်သာဆုံး
                            diversified
                                Pool အများကြီးမှာ
                                ခွဲသုံး
                            capacityOptimized
                                Server အများဆုံးကျန်တဲ့ Pool
                            priceCapacityOptimized
                                Capacity များတဲ့ Pool တွေထဲက
                                ဈေးအသက်သာဆုံးကိုရွေး
                            Note:
                                Lowest cost → ✅ lowestPrice
                                High availability / Reduce interruption risk → ✅ diversified
                                Lowest interruption rate → ✅ capacityOptimized
                                Best practice / Recommended for most workloads → ✅ priceCapacityOptimized
            Dedicated Host:
                a physical server with ec2 full dedicated.
                can use existing serverbound software(per-socket, per-core, vm license)
                Purchasing options:
                    on-demand: per second.
                    reserved: 1 or 3 yrs(no upfront, partial upfront, all upfront)
                most expensive option.
                using for complicated licensing model(BYOL)
            Dedicated Instances:
                no control over instance placement(hardware)
                may share hardware with other instances.
            Capacity Reservations:
                guarantee availability
                Reserve EC2 capacity, NOT reserve a discount.
                must pay normal On-Demand pricing.
                Capacity Reservation = Reserve servers (capacity)
                    Example
                        Capacity Reservation ဆိုတာ
                        EC2 server ကို
                        ကြိုတင်နေရာဦးထားတာ
                        ဖြစ်ပါတယ်။
                        ဥပမာ
                        မနက်ဖြန်
                        10 EC2
                        သေချာလိုတယ်ဆိုရင်
                        ဒီနေ့ကတည်းက
                        AWS ကို
                        "ဒီ server 10 လုံး
                        ငါ့အတွက် သိမ်းထားပါ"
                        လို့ပြောထားတာပါ။
    Private vs Public IP(IPv4)
        IP Address = House Address
        networking has two IPS: IPv4 and IPv6.
        IPv4 with three dots: 1.160.10.240
            still most common format.
            3.7 billion different addresss in public area.
            [0-255].[0-255].[0-255].[0-255]
        IPv6 very long: 3ffe:1900:4545:3:200:f8ff:fe21:67cf
            newer and solve problems for IOT.
        Private vs Public IP (IPv4) Fundamental Differences
            Public IP:
                Public IP means the machine can be identified on the internet (WWW) 
                    Public IP ဆိုတာ Internet ပေါ်ကနေ တိုက်ရိုက်ရှာတွေ့နိုင်တဲ့ IP 
                Must be unique across the whole web (not two machines can have the same public IP).
                Can be geo-located easily
            Private IP:
                Private IP means the machine can only be identified on a private network only
                    Private Network အတွင်းမှာပဲ အသုံးပြုတဲ့ IP
                The IP must be unique across the private network
                BUT two different private networks (two companies) can have the same IPs.
                    Because they are different private networks. They never see each other.
                Machines connect to WWW using a NAT + internet gateway (a proxy)
                    using flow:
                        Private EC2
                            │
                            ▼
                        NAT Gateway
                            │
                            ▼
                        Internet Gateway
                            │
                            ▼
                        Internet
                        Imagine:
                            Private EC2
                            10.0.1.10

                            It wants to install updates.

                            Destination: ubuntu.com

                            But

                            10.0.1.10
                            is private.

                            Internet doesn't know where it is.
                        NAT means: Network Address Translation
                            It changes:
                            Private IP
                            ↓
                            Public IP
                        Example
                            Before NAT
                            10.0.1.10

                            After NAT
                            54.12.90.8

                            Internet sees:
                            54.12.90.8

                            not
                            10.0.1.10
                        Without NAT
                            Private EC2
                            10.0.1.10
                            ↓
                            Internet
                            ❌ Doesn't know this IP
                        Important:
                            A Private EC2 can:
                            ✅ Access Internet (through NAT Gateway)

                            But
                            ❌ Internet cannot start a connection to the Private EC2.

                            This is why private subnets are much more secure.
            Memory:
                | Public IP                            | Private IP                                       |
                | ------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------ |
                | Reachable from Internet              | Not directly reachable from Internet             |
                | Globally unique                      | Only unique inside the private network           |
                | Used by Web Servers                  | Used by Databases, App Servers                   |
                | Can receive inbound Internet traffic | Needs NAT Gateway for outbound Internet access   |
                | Example: `54.x.x.x`                  | Example: `10.0.x.x`, `172.16.x.x`, `192.168.x.x` |
                Public IP
                    အိမ်လိပ်စာလိုပဲ

                    ကမ္ဘာပေါ်က လူတိုင်း
                    ရှာတွေ့နိုင်တယ်။
                Private IP
                    ကုမ္ပဏီအတွင်း
                    အခန်းနံပါတ်လိုပဲ

                    အပြင်က
                    မမြင်နိုင်ဘူး။
                NAT Gateway
                    Private IP ကို
                    Public IP အဖြစ်
                    ဘာသာပြန်ပေးတဲ့
                    Translator
                    NAT Gateway
                    =
                    Private → Internet ✅
                    Internet → Private ❌
            Elastic IPs:
                when stop/start ec2 instance -> change public ip.
                if need fixed IP, will use Elastic IP. it never changes until you release it.
                Elastic IP is pubilc IPv4 IP.
                can attach it to one instance.
                With an Elastic IP address, you can mask the failure of an instance or software by rapidly remapping the address to another instance in your account.
                    "mask the failure" means enduser don't even know the server changed.
                each aws account can have 5 Elastic IP by default. you can ask AWS to increase that.
                Overall, try to avoid using Elastic IP:
                    They often reflect poor architectural decisions.
                        Why is Elastic IP considered a poor architecture?
                            Imagine a company has:
                            100 EC2 Servers

                            Do you need:
                            100 Elastic IPs?

                            No.

                            That's difficult to manage.

                            AWS recommends:
                            One Load Balancer
                            ↓
                            Many EC2
                            instead.
                            Better Solution is
                                use a random public IP and register a DNS name to it.
                                    Example
                                        EC2:
                                        Public IP
                                        18.181.10.20

                                        Instead of telling users:
                                        18.181.10.20

                                        You create DNS:
                                        www.mycompany.com
                                        ↓
                                        18.181.10.20

                                        If Public IP changes,
                                        just update the DNS record.

                                        Users still type:
                                        www.mycompany.com

                                        They never notice.
                            Or, use a Load Balancer and don't use a public IP.
                            Example
                                Users
                                │
                                ▼
                                Load Balancer
                                │
                                ┌─┴───────────┐
                                ▼             ▼
                                EC2-1       EC2-2
                                Users never connect directly to EC2.
                                They connect only to the Load Balancer.
                                If EC2 fails,
                                the Load Balancer sends traffic to another EC2 automatically.
                                No need for Elastic IPs on each EC2.
                                Imagine Facebook.
                                    Do they give every server an Elastic IP?

                                    No.

                                    Millions of users connect to:
                                    facebook.com
                                    ↓
                                    DNS
                                    ↓
                                    Load Balancer
                                    ↓
                                    Thousands of servers
                Elastic IP = One stable address for one instance (or quickly moved to another instance).
                Load Balancer = One stable endpoint for many instances.
            private IP is for internal AWS Network.
            pubilc IP is for wwww.
            when we are doing SSH into ec2,
                we can't use private IP, because we are not same network.
                we can only use the public IP.
    Placement Groups:
        Placement Group ဆိုတာ
        EC2 Instance တွေကို
        AWS Data Center ထဲမှာ
        ဘယ်လိုနေရာချမလဲ
        ဆိုတာ သတ်မှတ်ပေးတဲ့ Strategy ဖြစ်တယ်။
        First, Why Do We Need Placement Groups?
            Normally, when you launch EC2 instances:

            EC2-1
            EC2-2
            EC2-3

            AWS decides where to place them.

            You don't control:

            Which physical server
            Which rack
            How close they are

            AWS automatically chooses.

            Sometimes, however, you have special requirements.

            For example:

            Requirement 1
            I want all EC2s
            to communicate
            VERY FAST.

            OR

            Requirement 2
            I don't want
            all EC2s to fail
            at the same time.

            AWS asks:

            "How should I place your EC2 instances?"

            That's exactly what Placement Groups are for.
        သတ်မှတ်ပေးတဲ့ Strategy: 3
            Cluster Placement Group
                Keyword: Performance
                aws pusts ec2: Very close together. communication becomes extremely fast.
                EC2 တွေကို
                တစ်နေရာတည်း
                အနီးနားမှာထားတာ။
                Disadvantage:
                    Imagine Rack A catches fire.
                    Everything fails together.
                    Higher performance,
                    Lower fault tolerance (အမှားခံနိုင်ရည်နည်းခြင်း)
                Note: Very safe.
            Spread Placement Group
                Keyword: Availability
                aws pusts ec2: separates EC2 instances.
                EC2 တွေကို
                တစ်နေရာတည်းမှာ မထားဘူး။
                ဝေးဝေးခွဲထားတယ်။
                Note: very safe.
            Partition Placement Group
                Each partition uses completely different racks.
                Partition Placement Group ဆိုတာ
                EC2 တွေကို
                အုပ်စုကြီးတွေခွဲထားတာ။
                Many Instances
                ↓
                One Partition
                Applications like:
                Hadoop, HDFS, HBase,
                Apache Kafka
                Cassandra,
                Hundreds of EC2
                already work in partitions.
                AWS aligns the hardware with the application design.
                Note: Scalable + fault isolation(ချို့ယွင်းချက်ခွဲထုတ်ခြင်း။)
            Memory:
                | Strategy      | Goal                               | Best For                    | Risk                                       | 
                | ------------- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ |
                | **Cluster**   | Lowest latency                     | HPC, ML, Trading            | One rack failure can affect many instances |
                | **Spread**    | Highest availability               | Critical applications       | Max 7 instances per AZ                     |
                | **Partition** | Fault isolation for large clusters | Hadoop, Cassandra, Kafka,Big Data| One partition can fail, others continue    |
                HPC means high-performance computing.
    Elastic Network Interfaces(ENI):
        "virtual network card".
        ENI ဆိုတာ EC2 ရဲ့ Network Card (LAN Card) လိုပဲ။
        Visual:
            Internet
                │
                ▼
            ENI
            (Virtual Network Card)
                │
                ▼
            EC2 Instance

            Without an ENI,
            the EC2 cannot communicate.
        Once you compare it to a physical computer,
        What is a Network Interface?
            Your laptop has a network card (NIC).
            It connects your laptop to the network.
            Without a network card,
            ❌ your laptop cannot communicate with other computers.
            EC2 is like a computer.
            It also needs a network card.
            AWS calls this network card: Elastic Network Interface.
        Why is it called "Elastic"?
            Because it is flexible.
            You can:
            Attach it
            Detach it
            Move it
            between EC2 instances (within the same Availability Zone).
        What does an ENI contain?
            Every ENI has exactly one Primary Private IPv4 address.
            one ENI owns multiple(Secondary) private IPs.
                Suppose your ENI has:
                    Primary
                    10.0.1.15

                    You can also add:
                    Secondary
                    10.0.1.20
                    10.0.1.21
                    10.0.1.22
                Why?
                    Example:
                    One EC2 hosts:
                    Website A
                    Website B
                    Website C

                    Each website uses a different IP.
                    One ENI can provide multiple private IPs.
            Security Group:
                When packets arrive,
                AWS checks the Security Group attached to the ENI.
            MAC Address:
                Just like your laptop has: 00:1A:2B...
                An ENI also has a MAC address.
            Public IP
                If assigned,
                the Public IP is associated with the ENI.
            Elastic IP
                You don't attach an Elastic IP directly to the EC2.
                You attach it to the ENI.
        Real Example
            Imagine:
                EC2 Web Server
            Attached ENI
                Private IP 10.0.1.10
                Elastic IP 52.10.10.10
                Security Group
                    HTTP
                    HTTPS
                    SSH
            Everything belongs to the ENI.
        Why Move an ENI?
            Imagine:
                EC2-1
                ↓
                ENI
            EC2-1 crashes.
            Create: EC2-2
            Detach ENI.
            Attach to EC2-2.
            Now EC2-2 immediately has:
            Same Private IP
            Same Elastic IP
            Same Security Groups
            Very useful for recovery.
        Can an EC2 Have Multiple ENIs?
            Yes!
            Example
                EC2
                ↓
                ENI-1
                ↓
                Private Network
            AND
                EC2
                ↓
                ENI-2
                ↓
                Management Network
            One EC2 can have multiple virtual network cards (the number depends on the instance type).
            Example:
                Imagine:
                    Application -> ENI-1 -> Web traffic
                    Application -> ENI-2 -> Database traffic
                Traffic is separated.
        Memory:
            Every EC2 must have at least one ENI.
            Every ENI has exactly one Primary Private IPv4 address.
            An ENI can also have multiple Secondary Private IPv4 addresses.
            An ENI can be detached from one EC2 and attached to another EC2 in the same Availability Zone.
            EC2 automatically creates a Primary ENI. 
                EC2 တစ်လုံးဖန်တီးလိုက်တာနဲ့ AWS က ENI ကိုလည်း အလိုအလျောက်ဖန်တီးပေးတယ်။
            An ENI can have multiple Private IPs (Primary + Secondary).
                inside of instance, when we check with cmd: ip addr <or> ip a
                secondary Private IPv4 will find.
            If EC2 are delete/terminate, auto-generated ENI also will delete because these are primary.
                Delete EC2. Primary ENI may be deleted. Secondary ENI can remain
            Primary ENI cannot be detached.
            How many ENIs can be attached?
                It depends on the instance type (t3.micro, m5.large, c5.xlarge, etc.).
                The number of ENIs and private IP addresses is determined by the EC2 instance type.
                For t3.micro
                    Maximum:
                    2 ENIs
                    Up to 2 IPv4 addresses per ENI
                Basically,
                    Larger instance types generally support:
                        More ENIs
                        More IP addresses
                        Higher network bandwidth
            Security Groups are attached to the ENI, not directly to the EC2 instance.
                ENI ထဲက Security Group ကို EC2 က အသုံးပြုနေတာ။
                Actually, AWS works like this:
                EC2
                │
                ▼
                Primary ENI
                │
                ▼
                Security Group
            ENI as the network identity of the EC2.
            Everything related to networking is attached to the ENI:
                ENI
                ├── Private IP
                ├── Secondary Private IPs
                ├── Elastic IP
                ├── MAC Address
                └── Security Groups
            The EC2 simply uses that network identity.
    Hibernate:
        3 important (lifecycle)actions:
            1. Stop
                After Stop
                    RAM
                    -------------
                    ❌ Cleared

                    EBS
                    -------------
                    ✅ Still Exists
                When Start again
                    AWS does:
                    Boot Operating System
                    ↓
                    Start Services
                    ↓
                    Start Application

                    Everything starts from the beginning.
                If EC2 is stopped,
                RAM is cleared.
                Redis cache disappears (unless configured otherwise).
                When EC2 starts,
                it must rebuild the cache. caches get warmed up, and that can take time!
                This causes slower startup.
                    Cache Warm-up:
                        our application uses Redis.
                            Initially

                            Cache

                            Empty

                            First user visits.

                            Database
                            ↓
                            Redis

                            Now cache becomes populated.
                            This is called Cache Warm-up.
            2. Hibernate
                Think of Windows.
                Instead of Shut Down,
                you click:
                Hibernate
                Computer powers off.
                BUT
                before shutting down,
                Windows saves RAM to disk.
                AWS does exactly the same.
                Before Hibernate
                    RAM
                        Application
                        Browser
                        Variables
                        Sessions
                    AWS copies RAM -> EBS
                    Then powers off.
                    When Start again
                        AWS does
                        Read RAM
                        ↓
                        Restore Memory
                        ↓
                        Continue
                        Application continues exactly where it stopped.
                Why is Hibernate faster?
                    Because OS is not stopped / restarted, aws perform: OS -> frozen(Pause) instead of shutdown.
                    Normal Start
                        Boot OS
                        ↓
                        Start Application
                        ↓
                        Load Cache
                        ↓
                        Reconnect DB
                        ↓
                        Ready
                        Maybe 60 seconds
                    Hibernate
                        Power On
                        ↓
                        Restore RAM
                        ↓
                        Continue
                        Maybe 15 seconds
                Is our application still running?
                    No.
                    Power is OFF.
                    CPU is OFF.
                    RAM has no power.
                    Nothing is executing.
                Why must Root EBS be encrypted?
                    Root EBS is the boot disk.
                        Imagine RAM contains
                            Password
                            API Keys
                            JWT Token
                            Database Password
                            User Session
                        AWS saves RAM
                        ↓
                        EBS

                        If EBS wasn't encrypted
                        someone with disk access could potentially read all those secrets.
            3. Terminate
                Terminate means
                    Destroy. RAM and Root EBS Deleted. Everything is gone.
        Compare All Three:
            Stop
                RAM ❌ Lost
                EBS ✅ Keep
            Hibernate
                RAM ✅ Saved (preserved=မပျောက်အောင် သိမ်းထားတာ)
                EBS ✅ Keep
            Terminate
                RAM ❌ Lost
                EBS ❌ Lost
        Memory:
            Hibernate ဆိုတာ
                RAM ထဲက Data ကို EBS ထဲမှာ သိမ်းထားပြီး စက်ပိတ်တာ။
                ပြန်ဖွင့်ရင် RAM ကို ပြန် Restoreလုပ်တယ်။
                Application က မ Run တော့ဘူး။
                CPU လည်း ပိတ်သွားတယ်။
                RAM လည်း Power မရှိတော့ဘူး။
            User Data is meant for initial setup(just only First start).
            Hibernate သုံးမယ်ဆို Root EBS Must be Encrypted ဖြစ်ရတယ်။
            Requirement:
                • Supported Instance Families - C3, C4, C5, 13, M3, M4, R3, R4, T2, T3, ...
                    Not supported Old Instance Type.
                    Not every EC2 supports Hibernate!!
                • Instance RAM Size - must be less than 150 GB.
                • Instance Size - not supported for bare metal instances.
                    Bare Metal EC2 က Physical Server ကို တိုက်ရိုက် အသုံးပြုတာ။
                    Hibernate က Virtualization နဲ့ အလုပ်လုပ်တဲ့ Feature ဖြစ်လို့ Support မလုပ်ဘူး။
                • AMI - Amazon Linux 2, Linux AMI, Ubuntu, RHEL, CentOS & Windows...
                • Root Volume - must be EBS, encrypted, not instance store, and Large enough.
                    Root EBS must have enough free space.
                    RAM က 32 GB ဆိုရင် Root EBS ထဲမှာ 32 GB ထက်ပိုတဲ့နေရာရှိရမယ်။ မရှိရင် Hibernate မရဘူး။
                • Available for On-Demand, Reserved and Spot Instances.
        HandsOnLab:
            Run cmd on ec2 console: uptime
            the ouput showing time is is OS up time.
            after Hibernate run, and recheck run cmd, uptime, same time will remain not start from 0 minute.
    Three Storage Types:
        Block Storage (EBS): it only knows Block. OS manage the folder/path.
        File Storage (EFS): it already know the folder/permissions/ownership think like Company Shared Folder.
        Object Storage (S3): s3 store Object not files, think like a warehouse.
        | Feature           | **Block Storage**   | **File Storage**        | **Object Storage**                    |
        | ----------------- | ------------------- | ----------------------- | ------------------------------------- |
        | AWS Service       | **EBS**             | **EFS**                 | **S3**                                |
        | Looks Like        | Hard Disk (SSD/HDD) | Shared Folder           | Cloud Bucket                          |
        | Stores            | Blocks              | Files & Folders         | Objects                               |
        | Folder Structure  | Created by OS       | Built-in                | Not a real filesystem (uses prefixes) |
        | Mount to EC2      | ✅ Yes              | ✅ Yes                 | ❌ No                                  |
        | Boot Volume       | ✅ Yes              | ❌ No                  | ❌ No                                  |
        | Share Between EC2 | Normally No         | Yes                     | Yes (via API)                         |
        | Access Method     | OS Disk Driver      | NFS                     | HTTP/API                              |
        | Typical Use       | OS, Database        | WordPress, Shared Files | Images, Videos, Backups, Logs         |
    EBS Volume:
        an EBS(Elastic Block Store) Volume is a network drive.
        Persist:
            Stop လုပ်လည်း Data မပျောက်ဘူး။
            For termination, remember it depends on the volume's Delete on Termination setting.
        can only be mounted to one instance at a time(at Cloud Practitioner (CCP) level)
            EBS တစ်လုံးကို တစ်ချိန်တည်းမှာ EC2 နှစ်လုံးမသုံးနိုင်ဘူး။
        scoped to a single Availability Zone./locked ta an az.
            when to move a volumn across, first need to snapshot it.
            EBS က
                AZ တစ်ခုနဲ့ ချိတ်ထားတာ။
                AZ-a မှာ ဖန်တီးထားရင်
                AZ-b က EC2 နဲ့ မချိတ်နိုင်ဘူး။
        as a "network USB sticks". but you Actually don't physically put it in a computer.
        Billed for all requested provisioned capacity.
            Provision means: "AWS allocates (reserves) resources for you in advance."
                Provisioned = ကြိုတင်သတ်မှတ်ပြီး AWS က သင့်အတွက် Resource ကို Reserve လုပ်ပေးထားတာ။
                    100 GB ဖန်တီးရင် 100 GB အတွက်ပေးရမယ်။
                    Example:
                        When creating an EBS volume, AWS asks:
                        Size:
                        [100 GB]
                        You choose: 100 GB
                        AWS immediately reserves 100 GB of storage for you.
                        Even if you only store one small file: hello.txt (1 MB)
                        and, AWS still charges for: 100 GB
                        because you reserved (provisioned) that capacity.
                IOPS = Input / Output Operations Per Second
                    Disk က တစ်စက္ကန့်မှာ ဘယ်နှစ်ကြိမ် Read / Write လုပ်နိုင်သလဲ။
            can increase capacity on later.
        Memory:
            EBS က EC2 ထဲမှာ တကယ်တပ်ထားတဲ့ Physical Hard Disk မဟုတ်ဘူး။ 
            AWS Network ကနေ ချိတ်ထားတဲ့ Disk ဖြစ်တယ်။ ဒါကြောင့် AWS က Network Drive လို့ခေါ်တာ။Everything is virtual.
            EBS vs Instance Store
                EBS is Network SSD.
                Instance Store is Physical SSD Inside Server.
    EBS Snapshot:
        make a backup of ebs volumn at a point in time.
            Point in Time ဆိုတာ အချိန်တစ်ခုမှာ ရှိတဲ့ Data အတိုင်း သိမ်းထားတာ။
            Example
                10:00 AM

                Database: 1000 Customers
                Take Snapshot.

                11:00 AM

                Database: 1100 Customers
                Restore snapshot.

                Result: 1000 Customers

                Not 1100.

                Because
                the snapshot captured 10:00 AM.
        not necessary to detach volumn, but recommended.
            for data consistency.
                Snapshot ရိုက်တဲ့အချိန် Application က Data ရေးနေတယ်ဆိုရင်
                Incomplete Data ဖြစ်နိုင်တယ်။
                Better Practice
                    For databases
                    People often:

                    Stop Database
                    ↓
                    Take Snapshot
                    ↓
                    Start Database
        Copy Across AZ.
            Snapshot lives in the Region.
            The Snapshot itself is regional.
            It is not tied to a single AZ.
                copy workflow in AZ:
                    EBS (AZ-a)
                    ↓
                    Snapshot (Region)
                    ↓
                    New EBS (AZ-b)
        Copy Across Region directly.
            AWS allows
                Snapshot
                ↓
                Copy
                ↓
                Singapore

                Then
                create a new EBS
                inside Singapore.
        Important Note:
            Description:
                "We don't know when an earthquake or disaster will happen. 
                So if we only take a snapshot every midnight, won't we lose today's data?"
                This leads us to one of the most important concepts
                    RPO (Recovery Point Objective)
                    RTO (Recovery Time Objective)
                    RPO means How much data loss can the business accept?
                    Suppose the company says:
                        Maximum Data Loss
                        24 hours
                        Daily snapshot is enough.
                    But if they say
                        Maximum Data Loss
                        15 minutes
                        Daily snapshot is NOT enough.
                    Therefore...
                    They take snapshots more frequently: Every Hour, Every 30 minutes.
                    But is Snapshot enough?
                    For many businesses...
                        NO.
            Business အရေးကြီးရင်
                နေ့တစ်ခါ မဟုတ်ဘဲ ၁ နာရီတစ်ခါ ၁၅ မိနစ်တစ်ခါ Snapshot ရိုက်ကြတယ်။
            ဒါပေမယ့် Bank လို Facebook လို Amazon Shopping လို
                System တွေက Snapshot တစ်ခုတည်းကို အားမကိုးဘူး။
                    Real-time Replication နဲ့
                    အခြား Region ကို အမြဲ Copy ကူးထားတယ်။
                "The company wants backups." => snapshot
                "The company wants almost zero data loss." =>
                    Snapshot alone is usually not enough.
                        You should think about:
                            Database replication
                            Multi-AZ
                            Cross-Region replication
                            Continuous backup (depending on the AWS service)
        Feature:
            EBS Snapshot Archive:
                75% cheaper:
                    Archive ဆိုတာ အသုံးမပြုတော့တဲ့ Snapshot ကို စျေးသက်သာတဲ့ Storage ထဲရွှေ့ထားတာ။
                    Why cheaper?
                    AWS doesn't keep it ready for immediate use.
                Takes within 24 to 72 hours(1 day to to 3 days) for restoring the archive
                    Question
                        Company must keep backups for 7 years at the lowest cost.
                    Answer
                        ✅ Snapshot Archive
            Recycle Bin:
                if accidentally click delete snapshot, aws store on Recycle bin as like as window.
                မှားဖျက်လိုက်ရင် ချက်ချင်းမပျောက်ဘူး။ but, must be "Create retention rule".
                Specify retention/keep data (from 1 day to 1 year)
                    Retention: Keep /ထိန်းသိမ်းမှု
            Fast Snapshot Restore:
                Without FSR:
                    if snapshot is 1 TB EBS, the first time read is slower.
                        AWS may need to fetch data from the snapshot in the background.
                        That initial access can be slower.
                        This is called lazy loading.
                        2nd time is faster.
                With FSR:
                    AWS prepares everything beforehand.
                        Every block is already initialized.
                        No waiting.
                        but, expensive.
                    When is FSR useful?
                        Example
                        Large production database.
                        10 TB
                        Disaster.
                        Need new database
                        NOW.
                        Cannot wait
                        for lazy loading.
                        Enable
                        Fast Snapshot Restore
                        Everything is immediately responsive.
            Question:
                A company creates new EBS volumes from snapshots during disaster recovery and 
                requires full performance immediately after creation.
                ✅ Answer: Fast Snapshot Restore (FSR)
    AMI:
        EC2 Template
        amazon machin image (AMI = Blueprint (Template) for creating EC2 instances.)
        customization of ec2 instance.
            can add own softwawre, configuration, os, monitor.
            faster boot/configuration time because all your software is pre-packaged.
        AMI are built for specific region. can be copied across regions.
            Question: You can use an AMI in N.Virginia Region us-east-1 to launch an EC2 instance in any AWS Region.
            Ans: False.
        can lauch ec2 from
            public ami, own ami, aws marketplace ami.
            public ami:
                created by AWS or the community.
            own ami:
                created by your company, Only your AWS account can use it.
            Marketplace AMI:
                Suppose you want:
                    Windows Server with SQL Server
                    Fortinet Firewall
                    Cisco Security Appliance
                Instead of installing everything yourself.
                include licensed software, so there may be additional charges.
        what contain:
            ├── Root EBS Snapshot
            ├── Operating System
            ├── Installed Software
            ├── Configuration
            ├── Boot Information
            ├── Block Device Mapping
            └── Metadata
        EC2 Instance Store:
            many beginners think: "All EC2 storage = EBS"
                ❌ Not true.
            AWS actually has 2 major storage types attached to EC2:
                1. EBS (Network Storage)
                2. Instance Store (Physical Storage)
            • EBS volumes are network drives with good but "limited" performance
            • If you need a high-performance hardware disk, use EC2 Instance Store.
                Because: The disk is physically attached to the host machine.
                example:
                    EBS is like as:
                        Your PC
                            ↓
                            Network
                            ↓
                            Storage
                        Because it is over the network:
                        Some latency exists.
                    Instance Store is like as:
                        Your PC
                        ↓
                        Internal SSD
                        no network, Very fast.
            • Better I/O performance
                Example:
                You are processing:
                10 TB logs
                or
                Millions of cache records
                or
                Temporary video rendering
                Need maximum speed.
                Instance Store can help.
            • EC2 Instance Store lose their storage if they're stopped (ephemeral)
                Ephemeral means temporary!
                Compare with EBS
                    EBS
                    Stop EC2
                    ↓
                    Data Survives

                    Instance Store
                    Stop EC2
                    ↓
                    Data Lost
            • Good for buffer / cache / scratch data / temporary content
                Never store: Production Database, Customer Data, Financial Records on Instance Store.
            • Risk of data loss if hardware fails
            • Backups and Replication are your responsibility.
                For EBS:
                    Snapshot
                    AWS helps.

                For Instance Store:
                    You handle backup
                    Your application must replicate data elsewhere.
            Summary:
                | Feature          | EBS (Elastic Block Store)               | Instance Store                 |
                | ---------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ |
                | Storage Type     | Network Storage                         | Physical Local Storage         |
                | Location         | Separate network storage                | Attached to EC2 host machine   |
                | Persistence      | ✅ Persistent                            | ❌ Temporary (Ephemeral)        |
                | Stop EC2         | ✅ Data remains                          | ❌ Data lost                    |
                | Terminate EC2    | Depends on DeleteOnTermination          | ❌ Data lost                    |
                | Snapshot Support | ✅ Yes                                   | ❌ No                           |
                | Backup           | Easy (Snapshots)                        | Manual                         |
                | Performance      | High                                    | Very High                      |
                | Hardware Failure | Protected by AWS                        | Data may be lost               |
                | Best For         | Databases, Applications, Important Data | Cache, Buffer, Temporary Files |

            Question: You are running a high-performance database that requires an IOPS of 310,000 for its underlying storage. 
                What do you recommend?
            Ans: EC2 Instance Store
            EBS with io1 is not enough becasue 64,000 is max IOPS on io1/io2.
        Reboot instance:
            When creating an AMI, AWS asks: ☑ Reboot instance
            What does it mean?
            Imagine you are editing a Microsoft Word file.
            Word File
                ↓
            You are typing
                ↓
            Not saved yet

            Now suddenly someone copies your hard disk.
            The copied file may be:
                Half saved
                Half not saved
            The data may be inconsistent.
            The same thing can happen on EC2.
            Suppose your EC2 is running:
                MySQL Database
                Apache
                Laravel
            Currently:
                RAM
                ↓
                Writing data
                ↓
                EBS Volume
            Some data may still be:
            RAM only
            and not yet written to disk.
            Reboot Instance = Checked
            ☑ Reboot instance
            AWS does:
            Stop Applications
            ↓
            Flush RAM to Disk
            ↓
            Reboot OS
            ↓
            Take Snapshot
            ↓
            Create AMI

            This creates a crash-consistent / file-system-consistent image.
            Much safer.

            Example
                Database Server
                MySQL
                Customer Orders

                Current order:
                Order #1001
                Still in memory.

                Not written to disk yet.
            If AWS takes snapshot immediately: Snapshot

            Maybe:
            Order #1000 exists
            Order #1001 missing
            because data wasn't fully written yet.

            If reboot is enabled:

            MySQL
            ↓
            Write everything to disk
            ↓
            Shutdown
            ↓
            Snapshot

            Now snapshot is consistent.

            Reboot Instance = Unchecked
            ☐ Reboot instance
            AWS does:
            Snapshot immediately
            without rebooting.
            Advantages:
            No downtime
            Faster
            Disadvantages:
            Risk of inconsistent data
            Real Example
            Suppose EC2 is:
                Apache Web Server
                Static HTML only.
                No database.
                No active writes.
            Then:
            ☐ Reboot instance
            is usually fine.
            Suppose EC2 is:
                MySQL Database Server
                or
                PostgreSQL
                or
                Oracle
            Then:
            ☑ Reboot instance

            is recommended.
            AWS Documentation Meaning
            AWS says:
            data is at rest
            Meaning:
            RAM
            ↓
            Disk
            Everything has already been written to disk.
        Note:
            If the question says:
                "The company wants to deploy hundreds of identical web servers quickly."
                Think:
                ✅ AMI
            Remember these relationships:
                EC2
                │
                ├── Root EBS Volume
                │         │
                │         └── EBS Snapshot (backup of the volume)
                │
                └── Create AMI
                        │
                        ├── Includes a snapshot of the root volume
                        └── Adds boot configuration and metadata
                                    │
                                    ▼
                                Launch New EC2
                Snapshots are primarily for backing up storage.
                AMIs are primarily for creating new EC2 instances quickly and consistently.
    EBS Volume Types:
        families:
            SSD Family
            ├── gp2
            ├── gp3
            ├── io1
            └── io2

            HDD Family
            ├── st1
            └── sc1
        First to understand SSD and HDD.
            SSD
                Advantages:
                    Fast
                    Low latency
                    High IOPS
                Best for:
                    OS
                    Database
                    Applications
            HDD
                Advantages:
                    Cheap
                    Large storage
                Best for:
                    Logs
                    Backups
                    Big files
        gp2/ gp3 (SSD): General purpose SSD volume. Default Choice.
            gp3 is basically the newer version. More performance, 
            Lower cost, cost effective storage, low-latency, develpment and test env.
        iol / io2 Block Express (SSD): Highest-performance premium SSD.
            mission-critical low-latency, databases workloads,
            support ebs multi-attach.
            think like:
                gp3: Normal SSD
                io2: Super SSD
            1000 IOPS = 1000 operations/sec.
        st1 (HDD): Low cost HDD volume/ frequently accessed, 
            throughput-intensive workloads/ big data/ data warehouses/ log processing.
                throughput = large amt of data.
                Example:
                    Video Files
                    Log Files
                    Analytics Data
                Need: High MB/sec
                not: High IOPS
                Use: ✅ st1 => Frequently Accessed Big Files.
        sc1 (HDD): Cheapest HDD volume/ less frequently accessed workloads.
            Data is rarely accessed
                Examples:
                    Archives
                    Old Logs
                    Old Reports
                Use: ✅ sc1 => Rarely Accessed Files.
        Important Note:
            gp2/gp3 and io1/io2 can be used as root/boot volumes.
            st1/sc1 cannot use as root/boot volume.
            Which EBS volume type should be used for a particular workload?
                Web Server
                EC2 + Apache

                Answer: ✅ gp3

                MySQL Database
                High Transaction Rate

                Answer: ✅ io2

                Video Streaming
                Large files

                Answer: ✅ st1

                Archive Storage
                Rarely accessed

                Answer: ✅ sc1
        EBS Multi-attach:
            Normally one EBS volume = one EC2 instance.
            But EBS Multi-Attach is a special exception.
            Limit 1: Only supported on: io1/io2
                Not other volumn types.
            Limit 2: Same AZ Only.
                Works/allowed:
                    EC2-1 (1a)
                    EC2-2 (1a)
                    EC2-3 (1a)
                    ↓
                    Same io2 Volume

                    Not allowed:
                        EC2-1 (1a)
                        EC2-2 (1b)
                        ↓
                        Same Volume
                    Because EBS volumes live inside a single AZ.
                Why would we need this?
                    Imagine:
                    Application Server A
                    Application Server B
                    Both need access to the same data.
                    Normal way:

                    Server A
                    ↓
                    Own Disk

                    Server B
                    ↓
                    Own Disk

                    Need synchronization.
                    Complicated.

                    Multi-Attach:
                    Server A
                        ↓
                        Shared Volume
                        ↑
                    Server B

                    Both read/write same storage.
                    Teradata(Clustered Database) is all need access to the same disk.
            Each instance has full read & write permissions.
                EC2-1: write file.txt
                Allowed.
                EC2-2: delete file.txt
                Allowed.
                This creates a problem. The Big Danger.
                Imagine:
                    EC2-1:
                    Writing customer.txt
                    At exactly same time:
                    EC2-2:
                    Writing customer.txt
                    Who wins?
                    Potential data corruption.
                    Therefore:
                    Applications must manage concurrent writes.
                    application must coordinate.
                        Example:
                            Server A
                            Wait
                            ↓
                            Write
                            ↓
                            Unlock
                            ↓
                            Server B Write
                            This is called: Locking.
        Up to 16 EC2 Instances at a time.
        Must use a file system that's cluster-aware (not XFS, EXT4, etc...)
            Because: EXT4 assumes: One Server owns the disk.
        Note:
            Question:
                A company wants to attach a gp3 volume to 5 EC2 instances.
                Answer:
                ❌ Not possible.
                Only:
                    io1
                    io2
                    support Multi-Attach.
            If the question says:
                "Multiple EC2 instances need shared storage"
                Don't immediately choose Multi-Attach.
                Ask yourself:
                Shared Block Storage?
                → EBS Multi-Attach
                Shared File Storage?
                → Later you'll learn Amazon EFS.
    EBS Encryption:
        if someone steals the disk: Unreadable.
        AWS does it automatically: Encryption and decryption are handled by AWS.
        Data at rest is encrypted inside the volume
            "Data at Rest" means: Stored on Disk.
        Data in Transit Encryption:
            All the data in flight moving between the instance and the volume 
            is encrypted
            Remember:
                EC2
                ↓
                Network
                ↓
                EBS

                Because EBS is network storage.

                Without encryption:
                EC2 ---> Data ---> EBS

                Someone intercepting traffic could potentially see it.

                With encryption:
                EC2 ---> Encrypted Data ---> EBS

                Much safer.
        All snapshots are encrypted Automatically if from Encrypted Volume.
        All volumes created from the snapshot.
            Example:
                Encrypted Volume
                ↓
                Encrypted Snapshot
                ↓
                New Volume
                Result: Encrypted Volume(cannot change Encryption checkbox.)
        can change encryption checkbox on creation from un-encrypted snapshot.
        Encryption has a minimal impact on latency by using AWS KMS.
    Amazon EFS(Elastic File System):
        is like a shared network folder.[EBS is like a hard disk for one computer]
        uses NFS Protocol.
            NFS uses: TCP Port 2049.
            NFS is a protocol that lets multiple Linux machines share files over the network.
            No NFS server installation required.
        Works Across Multiple AZs
            EBS can work single AZ.
            Question:Multiple EC2 instances in multiple Availability Zones need shared storage.
            ans: Amazon EFS
        use cases: cms, web serving, data sharing, wordpress
        uses securtiy group to control access to EFS.
        compatible with Linux based ami(not window)
            becasue:
                NFS is primarily a Linux/Unix protocol.
                Windows servers typically use SMB-based services such as Amazon FSx for Windows File Server.
        encryption at rest.
        Automatic Scaling
            Suppose today you store: 10 GB

            Tomorrow: 1 TB

            Next month: 20 TB

            Do you resize disks?

            ❌ No.

            EFS automatically grows.

            10 GB
            ↓
            100 GB
            ↓
            10 TB
            ↓
            100 TB

            Automatically.
        Pay-per-use:
            "Pay only for what you use"
            Unlike EBS,
            you don't choose:
            100 GB
            500 GB
            2 TB
            Instead,
            AWS measures: "Actual Usage"

            If you use: 5 GB

            You pay for: 5 GB
        No capacity planning.
            With EBS:
            You think:
            Should I create
            100 GB?
            200 GB?
            500 GB?
            With EFS:

            Create File System
            ↓
            Done
            AWS handles the storage growth.
        EFS Scale:
            Thousands of EC2 instances can access the same EFS simultaneously.
            You never resize EFS manually.
            AWS grows it automatically.
        EFS Performance Mode:
            General Purpose:
                fast response
                low latency
                good for normal websites.
            Max I/O:
                need: Thousands of servers reading files simultaneously.
                Very High Parallelism
                Higher Latency.
                Good for:
                    Big Data
                    Video Processing
                    Machine Learning
                    Analytics
                Higher Throughput(large amt of data)
                    Throughput Mode:
                        three modes:
                            Bursting(default): Good for Normal applications.
                            Provisioned: always needs 1 GB/sec.
                                good for Databases, High-performance applications, Predictable heavy workloads
                            Elastic:
                                If condition is Impossible to predict.
                                Elastic automatically changes throughput.
                                Good for Unpredictable workloads.
            Note:
                | Requirement                            | Choose              |
                | -------------------------------------- | ------------------- |
                | Web server, CMS, WordPress             | **General Purpose** |
                | Big Data, Analytics, Media Processing  | **Max I/O**         |
                | Normal workload with occasional spikes | **Bursting**        |
                | Constant guaranteed throughput         | **Provisioned**     |
                | Unpredictable workload                 | **Elastic**         |
        EFS Storage Classes:
            is very similar to Amazon S3 Storage Classes.
            three levels:
                Frequently Used/everyday files
                        ↓
                Standard

                Sometimes Used/accessed occasionally
                        ↓
                EFS-IA

                Almost Never Used/Rarely accessed/annual/ long-term retention
                        ↓
                Archive
            Lifecycle management:
                you don't move files manually.
                AWS does it automatically.
                example:
                    Suppose you configure:
                        30 Days
                        ↓
                        Move to IA

                        AWS checks:

                        No access for 30 days
                        ↓
                        Automatically move

                        No manual work.
            Availability:
                Multiple copies exist across Availability Zones.
                Example:
                    AZ-A
                    Copy
                    AZ-B
                    Copy
                    If one AZ fails,
                    EFS still works.
                    Perfect for production.
            One Zone:
                much cheaper.
                Good for:
                    Development
                    Testing
                    Temporary projects

                    Not ideal for production systems that require high availability.
            One Zone + IA:
                Very low cost.
                if using One Zone + IA, cost savings over 90%.
            Note:
                | Standard        | IA                    | Archive      |
                | --------------- | --------------------- | ------------ |
                | Frequent access | Infrequent            | Rare         |
                | Fast            | Slight retrieval cost | Cheapest     |
                | Highest price   | Lower price           | Lowest price |
                Question:
                Company stores project files rarely opened.
                Answer:✅ EFS-IA

                Question:
                Company stores audit files accessed once a year.
                Answer:✅ Archive
    Note:
        EBS vs EFS:
            | Feature                 | EBS                                                  | EFS                     |
            | ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
            | Storage Type            | Block Storage                                        | File Storage            |
            | Share with Multiple EC2 | Normally No (except Multi-Attach io1/io2 in same AZ) | Yes                     |
            | Multi-AZ                | No                                                   | Yes                     |
            | Auto Scaling            | No                                                   | Yes                     |
            | Snapshot                | Yes                                                  | Backup service          |
            | Linux                   | Yes                                                  | Yes                     |
