                | Main concept                 | Items & keys                      | **Nodes & relationships** |
                | Best for                     | Massive scalable application data | **Highly connected data** |
                | Serverless                   | ✅                                 | Managed                   |
                | Partition Key                | ⭐                                 | ❌                         |
                | Social network relationships | Possible, but not ideal           | ⭐⭐⭐                       |
                | Fraud relationship analysis  | Possible                          | ⭐⭐⭐                       |
                | Recommendation relationships | Possible                          | ⭐⭐⭐                       |
                | Knowledge graph              | ❌                                 | ⭐⭐⭐                       |
        Neptune Streams:
            Real-time stream of every change to graph data
            Changes available immediately after writing
            Strictly ordered
            No duplicates
            Accessible through HTTP REST API
            Used to:
            React to graph changes
            Send notifications
            Synchronize Neptune with S3 / OpenSearch / ElastiCache
            Replicate graph data across regions
            Note:
                | Question says...                          | Think...               |
                | ----------------------------------------- | ---------------------- |
                | **Real-time changes to Neptune graph**    | ⭐ Neptune Streams      |
                | **Ordered sequence of graph changes**     | ⭐ Neptune Streams      |
                | **React to graph changes**                | Neptune Streams        |
                | **Synchronize Neptune with S3**           | Neptune Streams        |
                | **Synchronize Neptune with OpenSearch**   | Neptune Streams        |
                | **Synchronize Neptune with ElastiCache**  | Neptune Streams        |
                | **Replicate Neptune data across regions** | Neptune Streams        |
                | **Graph change notifications**            | Neptune Streams        |
                | **MongoDB changes**                       | DocumentDB mechanisms  |
                | **DynamoDB item changes**                 | DynamoDB Streams       |
                | **S3 object events**                      | S3 Event Notifications |
    Keyspaces:
        fully managed, serverless, Apache Cassandra-compatible database service.
            designed for:
                Very large datasets
                Distributed systems
                High availability
                High write/read throughput
                Scaling across many machines
        Serverless.
        support Cassandra Query Language (CQL).
        Single-digit millisecond latency at scale
        Time-Series Data.
        Note:
            |                      | DynamoDB             | Amazon Keyspaces           |
            | -------------------- | -------------------- | -------------------------- |
            | Database type        | Key-value / document | Cassandra                  |
            | Compatibility        | AWS DynamoDB API     | **Apache Cassandra / CQL** |
            | Serverless           | ✅                    | ✅                          |
            | Highly scalable      | ✅                    | ✅                          |
            | Partition key        | ✅                    | Cassandra partition key    |
            | CQL                  | ❌                    | **✅**                      |
            | IoT                  | ✅                    | **✅**                      |
            | Cassandra migration  | ❌                    | **⭐**                      |
            | Multi-AZ replication | ✅                    | **3x across AZs**          |
            | PITR                 | 35 days              | 35 days                    |
    Timestream:
        fully managed, fast, scalable, time series database.
        time-series data.
        Trillions of Events
        sql support.
        Scheduled Queries
        Storage Tiering:
                            Timestream
                                │
                    ┌──────────┴──────────┐
                    ↓                     ↓
                Recent data          Historical data
                    ↓                     ↓
                Memory             Cost-optimized
                    store               storage
        Why not just use RDS?
            You could store time-based data in a relational database, 
            but huge volumes of continuously generated measurements can become expensive and difficult to scale.
            Imagine:
                Millions of IoT devices
                        ↓
                Thousands of measurements/sec
                        ↓
                Millions/billions of records
                        ↓
                    Timestream
        Use Cases:
            ├── IoT ⭐⭐⭐
            ├── Time-series data ⭐⭐⭐
            ├── Operational monitoring
            ├── Real-time analytics
            └── Application metrics
        |                                 | Timestream               | Keyspaces                         |
        | ------------------------------- | ------------------------ | --------------------------------- |
        | Main purpose                    | **Time-series database** | **Cassandra-compatible database** |
        | Database model                  | Time series              | Wide-column / Cassandra           |
        | Serverless                      | ✅                        | ✅                                 |
        | CQL                             | ❌                        | **✅**                             |
        | SQL                             | **✅**                    | CQL                               |
        | IoT                             | ⭐⭐⭐                      | ⭐⭐⭐                               |
        | Time-series                     | **⭐⭐⭐**                  | Possible                          |
        | Cassandra application           | ❌                        | **⭐⭐⭐**                           |
        | Historical time-based analytics | **⭐⭐⭐**                  | Possible                          |
    Note:
        | Database       | Serverless? | Key idea           |
        | -------------- | ----------- | ------------------ |
        | **DynamoDB**   | ✅ Yes       | Key-value/document |
        | **Timestream** | ✅ Yes       | Time-series        |
        | **Keyspaces**  | ✅ Yes       | Cassandra          |
        | **DocumentDB** | ❌ No*       | MongoDB-compatible |
        | **Neptune**    | ❌ No*       | Graph              |


        | Application / Requirement    | AWS Service    |
        | ---------------------------- | -------------- |
        | AWS-native serverless NoSQL  | **DynamoDB**   |
        | MongoDB application          | **DocumentDB** |
        | Graph / relationships        | **Neptune**    |
        | Apache Cassandra application | **Keyspaces**  |
Data & Analytics:
    Amazon Athena:
        Serverless SQL query over S3
        S3 = where my data/files are stored
        Athena = SQL engine that reads and queries those files
        supported file format:
            CSV
            JSON
            ORC
            Avro
            Parquet
        Parquet is especially important
            "Large amount of data in S3 and need to reduce the amount of data scanned by Athena"
            Think about columnar formats such as Parquet or ORC
        $5 per TB scanned
        Reduce data scanned → reduce Athena cost
            Common techniques include:
                Partition your data
                Use Parquet/ORC
                Select only required columns
                Compress data
        To Performance Improvement:
            use columnar data for cost-savings(less scan)
                Apache Parquet or ORC is recommended.
                Huge performance Improvement
                Use Glue to convert your data to Parquet or ORC
                    AWS Glue:
                        CSV files
                        │
                        ▼
                        AWS Glue
                        │
                        ▼
                        Parquet / ORC
                        │
                        ▼
                        Amazon S3
                        │
                        ▼
                        Athena
            Compress data for smaller retrievals
                compression formats include:
                    GZIP
                    Snappy
                    ZSTD
            Partition datasets
            use Larger files (>128 MB) to minimize overhead.
                to avoid lots of tiny files.
            goal: Scan less → retrieve less → pay less → query faster
        Athena Federated Query:
            Normal Athena: Athena → query data in S3
            Federated Query: Athena → query data in different data sources
            Those sources can be:
                Relational databases
                NoSQL databases
                Object stores
                Custom data sources
                AWS services
                On-premises data sources
            using Data Source Connector: Lambda.
                Athena
                │
                │ SQL
                ▼
                Lambda Data Source Connector
                │
                ▼
                DynamoDB

                |                   | Normal Athena          | Federated Query  |
                | ----------------- | ---------------------- | ---------------- |
                | Main data source  | **S3**                 | Multiple sources |
                | Query language    | SQL                    | SQL              |
                | Serverless        | ✅                      | ✅                |
                | Lambda connectors | Usually no             | **Yes**          |
                | RDS               | Not directly as source | ✅                |
                | DynamoDB          | Not directly as source | ✅                |
                | CloudWatch Logs   | Not directly as source | ✅                |
                | On-premises data  | Not directly           | ✅                |
                | Results           | S3                     | S3               |
        use cases:
            Athena + QuickSight:
                for reporting/dashboards
                            S3
                            │
                            │ data
                            ▼
                            Athena
                            │
                            │ SQL results
                            ▼
                        QuickSight
                            │
                            ▼
                    Dashboards / Reports
                exam context: "Company stores data in S3 and wants to create interactive BI dashboards without managing servers."
            Athena and AWS Logs:
                for analyzing logs stored in S3
        Important:
            Athena + reduce query cost/data scanned → partitioning + Parquet/ORC
            Athena does NOT store your data. S3 stores it; Athena queries it.

            "Serverless SQL queries against data stored in S3"
            ➡️ Athena

            "Analyze S3 data without loading it into a database"
            ➡️ Athena

            "Query VPC Flow Logs stored in S3"
            ➡️ Athena

            "Analyze CloudTrail logs in S3 using SQL"
            ➡️ Athena

            "Create BI dashboards from Athena results"
            ➡️ Athena + QuickSight

            "Reduce Athena query costs"
            ➡️ Partition data + columnar formats such as Parquet/ORC

            "Data warehouse for large-scale analytics"
            ➡️ Redshift, not Athena.
    Amazon Redshit:
        fully managed data warehouse designed for OLAP workloads.
        OLTP:
            Online Transaction Processing:
                Customer buys something
                    ↓
                INSERT order
                UPDATE inventory
                UPDATE payment
            Characteristics:
                Many small transactions
                Frequent INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE
                Low-latency individual operations
                Application database
            Examples: RDS / Aurora / DynamoDB
        OLAP:
            Online Analytical Processing:
                "Show me total sales by country for the last 5 years."
            Characteristics:
                Large datasets
                Complex queries
                Aggregations
                Joins
                Reporting
                Business intelligence
                Data warehouse
        Redshift is based on PostgreSQL.
        Columnar storage:
            Traditional relational databases often store data by row.
            Redshift uses columnar storage.
        Parallel query engine
                          Redshift
                            │
                    ┌─────────┼─────────┐
                    ▼         ▼         ▼
                Node 1    Node 2    Node 3
                    │         │         │
                data      data      data
                    │         │         │
                    └─────────┼─────────┘
                            ▼
                        Result
        Two deployment modes:
            Provisioned Redshift:
                You choose the capacity.
                    Redshift Cluster
                    ├── Compute
                    └── Storage
            Serverless cluster:
                SQL Query
                ↓
                Redshift Serverless
                ↓
                AWS manages compute
        SQL interface
        BI tools
            integrates with BI tools such as:
                Amazon QuickSight
                Tableau
                Power BI
                other SQL/BI tools
        Redshift vs Athena:
            |                        | **Athena**            | **Redshift**                       |
            | ---------------------- | --------------------- | ---------------------------------- |
            | Main purpose           | Query data in S3      | Data warehouse                     |
            | Storage                | S3                    | Redshift managed warehouse storage |
            | Serverless             | ✅                     | ✅ Redshift Serverless              |
            | SQL                    | ✅                     | ✅                                  |
            | OLAP                   | ✅                     | ⭐⭐⭐                                |
            | Complex joins          | Good                  | **Excellent**                      |
            | Complex aggregations   | Good                  | **Excellent**                      |
            | Large repeated queries | Good                  | **Better**                         |
            | Data warehouse         | ❌                     | ✅                                  |
            | Columnar               | Can query Parquet/ORC | **Native columnar architecture**   |
            | Data must be in S3?    | Normally yes          | No                                 |
            | BI                     | QuickSight etc.       | QuickSight/Tableau etc.            |
        Snapshots:
            point-in-time backup of a Redshift cluster.
            incremental
                after the initial snapshot, subsequent snapshots save changed data rather than repeatedly copying everything.
            automatically takes snapshots:
                with schedule:
                    Every 8 hours OR after 5 GB per node of data changes — whichever comes first.
            set Retention.
                default: 1 day.
        Restore Snapshot:
            can be restored into a new Redshift cluster.
        Disaster Recovery:
            can configure snapshots to be copied to another AWS Region.
        Multi-AZ
        exam summary:
            | Question clue                         | Think                            |
            | ------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- |
            | Point-in-time backup                  | **Redshift Snapshot**            |
            | Automatic backups                     | **Automated snapshots**          |
            | User-created backup                   | **Manual snapshot**              |
            | Restore Redshift after failure        | **Snapshot → new cluster**       |
            | AZ failure, continue operating        | **Multi-AZ**                     |
            | Region failure / DR                   | **Cross-Region snapshot copy**   |
            | Keep an important backup indefinitely | **Manual snapshot**              |
            | Automatically remove old backups      | **Automated snapshot retention** |
        how data gets into Redshift efficiently?
            Large INSERTs are MUCH better. 
            don't treat like an OLTP database
                Redshift ကို တစ်ကြောင်းချင်း INSERT မလုပ်သင့်
                "Data အများကြီးပေးပါ။ တစ်ခါတည်း analyze လုပ်မယ်။"
                Batch လုပ်ပြီး ထည့်တာ ပိုကောင်း
            Kinesis Data Firehose → Redshift
                Firehose က Redshift ကို record တစ်ခုချင်းစီ မပို့ဘူး။
                Firehose က data ကို buffer / batch လုပ်တယ်။
                ပြီးတော့ S3 ကို staging area အဖြစ်သုံးပြီး Redshift ကို load လုပ်တယ်။
                    Applications
                        │
                        │ streaming data
                        ▼
                    Kinesis Data Firehose
                        │
                        │ buffer / batch
                        ▼
                        S3
                        │
                        │ COPY
                        ▼
                    Redshift
                "Redshift က S3 နဲ့ communicate လုပ်တဲ့ traffic ကို ဘယ် network path ကနေသွားစေချင်လဲ?"
                    Without Enhanced VPC Routing:
                        normal.
                            S3
                            │
                            │ AWS internal path
                            ▼
                            Redshift
                    With Enhanced VPC Routing:
                        Redshift က S3 နဲ့ data transfer လုပ်တဲ့ traffic ကို VPC ထဲက network path ကနေ route လုပ်တယ်။
                        ဒါကြောင့် VPC-level network controls ကို အသုံးချနိုင်တယ်။
                EC2 + JDBC driver → Redshift:
                    Java/application code can connect to Redshift using a JDBC driver and execute SQL.
                    application က: 100 recordsကို စုထားပြီး: EC2 -> Batch -> Redshift လိုမျိုး ပို့တာက ပိုကောင်းတယ်။
                    COPY vs INSERT:
                        INSERT : Good for Small amount of data
                        COPY : Good for Large datasets
                    So, Large data in S3 → COPY
                    prefer Batch because OLAP data warehouse.

                Athena: S3 ထဲက data ကို query လုပ်ချင်တယ်
                Redshift: Data warehouse ထဲကို data load လုပ်ပြီး heavy analytics လုပ်ချင်တယ်
                | Requirement                          | Best thing to think of       |
                | ------------------------------------ | ---------------------------- |
                | Large data → Redshift                | **Batch loading**            |
                | S3 → Redshift                        | **COPY command**             |
                | Streaming → Redshift                 | **Kinesis Data Firehose**    |
                | Firehose → Redshift                  | **Firehose → S3 → Redshift** |
                | Many small INSERTs                   | ❌ Avoid                      |
                | Large/batched INSERTs                | ✅ Better                     |
                | EC2 application → Redshift           | **JDBC**                     |
                | Keep S3/Redshift traffic through VPC | **Enhanced VPC Routing**     |
        Redshift Spectrum:
            Redshift ကနေ S3 ထဲက data ကို မရွှေ့ဘဲ query လုပ်ခြင်း
            Normally, S3 -> COPY -> Redshift.
            Data အရမ်းများရင် Storage လည်း Redshift မှာ လိုမယ်။
            ဒီအခြေအနေမှာ Redshift Spectrum သုံးနိုင်တယ်။
            Spectrum က S3 ထဲက data ကို တိုက်ရိုက် query လုပ်ပေးတယ်။
            requirement:
                Must have a Redshift cluster
                Spectrum ကို သီးသန့် standalone query service အနေနဲ့ မသုံးဘူး။
                            Redshift Cluster
                                │
                                ▼
                        Redshift Spectrum
                                │
                                ▼
                                S3
                unlike Athena:
                        S3
                        │
                        ▼
                        Athena
                        │
                        ▼
                        SQL
            query is submitted to Thousands of Redshift Spectrum nodes
                S3 data ကို parallel အနေနဲ့ Redshift architecture နဲ့ analyze လုပ်ပေး
            |                                       | Athena           | Redshift Spectrum         |
            | ------------------------------------- | ---------------- | ------------------------- |
            | Query S3 data                         | ✅                | ✅                         |
            | Data ကို S3 မှာထား                    | ✅                | ✅                         |
            | Data ကို Redshift ထဲ load လုပ်ရန်လို? | ❌                | ❌                         |
            | Redshift cluster လို?                 | ❌                | **✅**                     |
            | SQL                                   | ✅                | ✅                         |
            | Serverless query service              | **✅**            | ❌                         |
            | Redshift နဲ့ integrate                | Optional         | **Core purpose**          |
            | Main idea                             | S3 ကို SQL query | Redshift ကနေ S3 ကို query |

            Athena → S3 ကို query
            Redshift Spectrum → Redshift ကနေ S3 ကို query
            Mainly use for: data warehouse + data lake.
    OpenSearch (ex: ElasticSearch):
        searching နဲ့ log/data analysis အတွက်သုံးတဲ့ managed service
        DynamoDB = exact key နဲ့ data ရှာတာကောင်း
        OpenSearch = text / field / partial match နဲ့ data ရှာတာကောင်း(full-text search / partial matching)
        Elasticsearch ရဲ့ successor
        OpenSearch ကို Database အစားသုံးတာမဟုတ်ဘူး
            DynamoDB က source of truth ဖြစ်နိုင်ပြီး OpenSearch က search index ဖြစ်နိုင်
        Two modes:
            Managed clusters:
                ကိုယ်တိုင် cluster resources ကို configure/manage လုပ်ရ
            OpenSearch Serverless:
                Infrastructure / capacity ကို AWS က manage လုပ်ပေး
        OpenSearch Dashboards
        Note:
            DynamoDB
                ↓
            "Give me item with this KEY"

            OpenSearch
                ↓
            "Find documents matching this TEXT"

            Athena
                ↓
            "Run SQL on files in S3"

            Redshift
                ↓
            "Run heavy OLAP analytics on a warehouse"

            OpenSearch Dashboards
                ↓
            "Visualize OpenSearch data"
    Amazon EMR(Elastic MapReduce):
        Big Data processing / analytics အတွက် အသုံးပြုတဲ့ managed service
        idea: 
            Data အရမ်းများတယ် → machine တစ်လုံးတည်းနဲ့ မနိုင်ဘူး → EC2 machines အများကြီးကို cluster လုပ် → data ကို parallel processing လုပ်
                          Huge Data
                                │
                                ▼
                        ┌───────────────────┐
                        │    Amazon EMR     │
                        │    Cluster        │
                        └───────────────────┘
                        │   │   │   │
                        ▼   ▼   ▼   ▼
                        EC2 EC2 EC2 EC2
                        │   │   │   │
                        └───┴───┴───┘
                                │
                                ▼
                            Processed Data
            EC2 instance hundreds အထိပါဝင်တဲ့ cluster တွေတည်ဆောက်နိုင်ပါတယ်။
        Hadoop ecosystem ရဲ့ tools/frameworks အများကြီးကို support လုပ်
            Hadoop = Data အများကြီးကို computer အများကြီးနဲ့ ခွဲပြီး process လုပ်တဲ့ system
        EMR က ဘာကို Manage လုပ်ပေးလဲ
            သာမန် EC2 နဲ့ Hadoop cluster ကို ကိုယ်တိုင်လုပ်မယ်ဆိုရင်—
                EC2 create
                ↓
                Networking
                ↓
                Install Hadoop
                ↓
                Configure Hadoop
                ↓
                Install Spark
                ↓
                Configure Spark
                ↓
                Configure cluster
                ↓
                Monitor
                ↓
                Scale
                အလုပ်တွေ အများကြီးရှိပါတယ်။
            EMR သုံးရင်—
                You
                ↓
                Create EMR Cluster
                ↓
                AWS EMR
                ├── Provision instances
                ├── Install software
                ├── Configure cluster
                ├── Manage cluster
                └── Scaling
            ဆိုပြီး AWS က အများကြီး manage ပေးပါတယ်။
        bundled with Apache Spark, HBase, Presto, Flink...
        EMR က cluster ကို workload အလိုက် scale up / scale down လုပ်နိုင်
            ဒါကြောင့် cost နဲ့ performance ကို balance လုပ်နိုင်ပါတယ်။
        EMR + Spot Instances:
            "Instance ပျက်သွားရင် job ကို retry လုပ်နိုင်တယ်"
            လိုမျိုး fault-tolerant ဖြစ်နိုင်ပါတယ်။
            ဒီလို workload တွေအတွက် Spot Instances သုံးပြီး cost လျှော့နိုင်ပါတယ်။
            Big Data processing + cost optimization
            အချိန်တိတိကျကျ မပျက်မကွက် run ရမယ့် workload တွေအတွက် Spot ကို မသုံးသင့်
        Node Types:
            Master:
                Cluster ရဲ့ manager
                main management.
                Long-running:
                    stable instance သုံးတာက ပိုသင့်တော်ပါတယ်။
            Core:
                Task + Data Storage
                two jobs: Core Node
                            ├── Run processing tasks
                            └── Store data
                Long-running:
                    ပိုပြီး stable purchasing option သုံး
                        Core node မှာ data storage ပါတဲ့အတွက် Spot interruption ဖြစ်ရင် cluster/data processing ကို ထိခိုက်နိုင်ပါတယ်။
            Task:
                Compute only
                ဘာကြောင့် Task Node ကို Spot သုံးလေ့ရှိလဲ?
                    Task Node
                        ↓
                    Spot Instance
                        ↓
                    Cheap 💰
                        ↓
                    Terminated?
                        ↓
                    Other nodes continue
                "Need additional compute capacity for EMR and can tolerate interruptions."
        Note:
            EMR = Process Big Data with Spark/Hadoop

            | Node       | အဓိကတာဝန်            | Storage | Typical role          |
            | ---------- | -------------------- | ------- | --------------------- |
            | **Master** | Cluster management   | —       | Stable / long-running |
            | **Core**   | Tasks + data storage | ✅       | Stable / long-running |
            | **Task**   | Tasks only           | ❌       | Often Spot            |
    QuickSight:
        AWS ရဲ့ Business Intelligence (BI) service
        → Database / S3 / Athena / Redshift စတဲ့ data တွေကိုယူပြီး chart, graph, dashboard တွေတည်ဆောက်ပြီး
        business insight ရှာဖို့သုံးတာ။
        QuickSight
            │
            ├── Charts
            ├── Graphs
            ├── Analysis
            └── Dashboards
        Interactive Dashboard:
            user က filter / date range / region စတာတွေ ပြောင်းပြီး data ကို dynamically ကြည့်နိုင်
        Ad-hoc Analysis:
            ကြိုတင်သတ်မှတ်ထားတဲ့ report မဟုတ်ဘဲ လိုအပ်တဲ့အချိန်မှာ မေးခွန်းအသစ်တစ်ခုကို ချက်ချင်း data ခွဲခြမ်းစိတ်ဖြာတာ။
            Ad-hoc = “I have a new question. Let me analyze the data now.”
        SPICE:
            “Import data into QuickSight for fast in-memory analysis.”
            QuickSight ရဲ့ in-memory data engine ပါ။
                Data Source
                    │
                    │ Import
                    ▼
                    SPICE
                    │
                    ▼
                    QuickSight
                    │
                    ▼
                    Fast Analysis
            SPICE ထဲကို data import လုပ်လိုက်ရင်
            Database ကို query တိုင်းတိုင်း မလုပ်ဘဲ SPICE ထဲက data ကို memory ထဲမှာ process လုပ်နိုင်တာကြောင့်
            analysis/dashboard ကို မြန်မြန်လုပ်နိုင်
        Automatically scalable
        Embeddable
            QuickSight dashboard ကို ကိုယ့် application ထဲမှာ ထည့်ပြနိုင်
        Per-session pricing
            user တစ်ယောက် dashboard ကို အသုံးပြုတဲ့ session အပေါ်မူတည်ပြီး charge လုပ်တဲ့ pricing model
        Column-Level Security (CLS):
            User တစ်ယောက်ချင်းစီကို column တချို့ပဲ မြင်ခွင့်ပေးတာ
        Row-Level Security (RLS):
            user တစ်ယောက်ချင်းစီ မြင်နိုင်တဲ့ data rows ကို ကန့်သတ်နိုင်
        QuickSight Users & Groups ≠ IAM
            QuickSight ထဲမှာ user တွေကို သီးခြား manage လုပ်နိုင်
            QuickSight User/Group တွေဟာ QuickSight အတွင်းက identity ဖြစ်ပြီး IAM User/Group မဟုတ်
            Enterprise Edition မှာ Groups တွေကို အသုံးပြုနိုင်
        Analysis:
            Dashboard မထုတ်ခင် ကိုယ်တိုင် data ကို analyze/design လုပ်နေတဲ့ workspace
            dashboard ကို တည်ဆောက်/ပြင်ဆင် နေ
            can share the analysis or the dashboard with Users or Groups
        Dashboard:
            Analysis ပြီးသွားပြီဆိုရင်—
                Analysis
                ↓ Publish
                Dashboard
            Dashboard က အခြားသူတွေကို ကြည့်ရှုဖို့ share လုပ်နိုင်တဲ့ published version ဖြစ်ပါတယ်။
            Analysis ရဲ့ published/read-only version
            To share a dashboard, you must first publish it.
        Analysis vs Dashboard:
            |                       | Analysis        | Dashboard            |
            | --------------------- | --------------- | -------------------- |
            | Purpose               | Analyze / build | View / share         |
            | Edit                  | ✅               | Generally read-only  |
            | Charts ပြင်           | ✅               | ❌                    |
            | Filters/configuration | Build           | Preserved            |
            | Share                 | ✅               | ✅                    |
            | Publish လို?          | —               | **Yes**              |
            | Mental model          | **Workspace**   | **Published report** |
    Amazon Glue:
        Serverless
        Managed extract, transform, and load (ETL) service
        အဓိက use case က Data ကို analytics မလုပ်ခင် prepare/transform လုပ်ခြင်း
        Glue Job Bookmarks:
            “Don't process the same old data again.”
                ပြီးခဲ့တဲ့ run မှာ process လုပ်ပြီးသား data ကို နောက်တစ်ခါ ပြန်မ process လုပ်အောင် track လုပ်ပေးတာ
        Glue DataBrew:
            Data cleaning/preparation with pre-built transformations
                Code ရေးတာထက် GUI နဲ့ data ကို clean/normalize လုပ်ချင်တယ်
        Glue Studio:
            Build ETL Job
            Glue ETL jobs တွေကို GUI နဲ့ create/run/monitor လုပ်နိုင်တဲ့ visual interface
        Glue Streaming ETL:
            ပုံမှန် ETL က batch processing လုပ်လေ့ရှိပါတယ်
            ဒါပေမယ့် data က continuous stream အနေနဲ့ ဝင်လာရင် Streaming ETL သုံးနိုင်ပါတယ်။
        Apache Spark Structured Streaming:
            Continuous streaming data ကို Spark နဲ့ process/transform လုပ်ခြင်း
            data sources:
                Kinesis Data Streams
                Kafka
                Amazon MSK(Managed Streaming for Apache Kafka)
    AWS Lake Formation:
        Data အမျိုးမျိုးကို S3-based Data Lake တစ်ခုထဲ စုစည်းပြီး၊ data ကို
        clean/catalog/secure/access-control လုပ်ပေးတဲ့ managed service
                         Lake Formation
                                    │
                    ┌────────────┼────────────┐
                    ▼            ▼            ▼
                    Collect       Clean       Catalog
                    │            │            │
                    └────────────┼────────────┘
                                    ▼
                                Secure
                                    │
                                    ▼
                                Data Lake
            Data Lake = central place to have all your data for analytics purposes
                Data Lake ထဲမှာ—
                    Structured data → database tables
                    Semi-structured → JSON
                    Unstructured → images, documents, logs
                    တွေကို အတူတူထားနိုင်
                is not separate storage service, S3 bucket can serve as Data Lake.
            Data Lake = raw + structured + unstructured data အများကြီးကို စုထား
            Data Warehouse = analytics အတွက် structured data ကို စနစ်တကျထား
        Central Permission Management:
            Row-level = which records can you see?
            Column-level = which fields can you see?
        Lake Formation + S3:
                    Data Sources
                ┌─────┬─────┬─────┐
                ▼     ▼     ▼     ▼
                RDS  DynamoDB IoT Logs
                │     │     │     │
                └─────┴─────┴─────┘
                            │
                            ▼
                    Lake Formation
                            │
                            ▼
                            S3
                        Data Lake
                                │
                    ┌─────────┼─────────┐
                    ▼         ▼         ▼
            Athena    Redshift   QuickSight
            S3 က Data Lake ရဲ့ storage layer ဖြစ်နိုင်ပြီး၊
            Lake Formation က Data Lake ကို setup/govern/secure လုပ်တဲ့ management layer
        Structured + Unstructured Data:
            Structured
            └── RDS customer table

            Semi-structured
            └── JSON application events

            Unstructured
            ├── PDF
            ├── Images
            └── Logs
        Discover, Cleanse, Transform, Ingest:
            Discover: ဘယ် data တွေရှိလဲ ရှာဖွေခြင်း
            Cleanse: မလိုအပ်တာတွေဖယ်၊ မမှန်တာပြင်
            Transform: Data format/structure ကို ပြောင်းခြင်း
            Ingest: Source ကနေ Data Lake ထဲကို data သွင်းခြင်း
        Fine-grained access control:
            Data ကို အကြမ်းဖျင်း “table တစ်ခုလုံး” access ပေးတာမဟုတ်ဘဲ row / column အဆင့်အထိ ခွင့်ပြုချက်ကန့်သတ်နိုင်တာ
        Built on top of AWS Glue:
                        Lake Formation
                            │
                            ▼
                            Glue
                            │
                    ┌──────────┼──────────┐
                    ▼          ▼          ▼
                Catalog      ETL       Crawlers
        Glue vs Lake Formation
            |                      | AWS Glue                 | AWS Lake Formation                  |
            | -------------------- | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------- |
            | ETL                  | ⭐ Main                   | Supports                            |
            | Clean/Transform      | ⭐                        | Supports                            |
            | Data Catalog         | ✅                        | Uses Glue                           |
            | Data Lake setup      | —                        | ⭐ Main                              |
            | Data Lake governance | —                        | ⭐ Main                              |
            | Fine-grained access  | Limited context          | ⭐ Row/Column                        |
            | Mental model         | **ETL/Data preparation** | **Data Lake management & security** |
        ML Transforms / De-duplication:
        Source Blueprints:
            Lake Formation မှာ data source တွေကနေ Data Lake ထဲ data ထည့်ဖို့ pre-built source blueprints
    Amazon Managed Service for Apache Flink:
        Apache Flink နဲ့ streaming data ကို process လုပ်ဖို့
        old name: Kinesis Data Analytics for Apache Flink
        Apache Flink:
            stream processing framework
            Flink က data အားလုံးကို စုစုပေါင်းပြီး နောက်နေ့မှ process လုပ်တာမျိုးမဟုတ်ဘဲ stream ဝင်လာရင်း process လုပ်နိုင်ပါတယ်။
        Provisioned Compute Resources
            not 
        Parallel Computation
        Automatic Scaling
        Checkpoints & Snapshots
            Checkpoint:
                application state ရဲ့ recovery point
                Flink application က streaming data ကို process လုပ်နေတုန်း failure ဖြစ်သွားနိုင်
                Application crash ဖြစ်ရင် အစကနေ data အားလုံးကို ပြန်လုပ်မယ့်အစား checkpoint ကနေ recovery လုပ်နိုင်
            Snapshot:
                application state ကို သိမ်းထားတဲ့ longer-term/reusable save point
        Important:
            Flink does NOT read from Firehose
                Firehose ရဲ့ အဓိကအလုပ်က streaming data ကို destination ဆီ delivery လုပ်ပေးတာ
            Flink ရဲ့ streaming input အနေနဲ့ Kinesis Data Streams ကို သုံးနိုင်
        Glue Streaming ETL vs Managed Flink:
            Glue Streaming ETL
                Kinesis Data Streams
                        ↓
                Glue Streaming ETL
                        ↓
                Transform
                        ↓
                Destination
                Glue Streaming ETL က ETL/data preparation ကို အဓိကထားပါတယ်။

            Managed Service for Apache Flink
                Kinesis Data Streams
                        ↓
                Managed Flink
                        ↓
                Flink Application
                        ↓
                Complex Stream Processing
                ပိုပြီး custom/advanced stream processing logic ရေးချင်တဲ့အခါ Flink ကို စဉ်းစားပါ။

                Glue Streaming ETL = Streaming ETL
                Managed Flink = Apache Flink stream processing

                Kinesis Family:
                                 KINESIS
                                        │
                            ┌─────────┴─────────┐
                            ▼                   ▼
                    Data Streams            Firehose
                            │                   │
                            ▼                   ▼
                    Flink / Glue          Delivery
                    processing            to S3,
                                            Redshift,
                                            OpenSearch...
                Apache Flink → Managed Service for Apache Flink
                Kinesis Data Streams → Flink = ✅
                Kinesis Data Firehose → Flink = ❌
        Amazon MSK (Managed Streaming for Apache Kafka):
            Apache Kafka:
                a platform for streaming data.
                Kafka is especially useful when many applications
                    need to consume the same continuous stream of events.
                MSK vs Kinesis:
                    |                             | Kinesis Data Streams | Amazon MSK       |
                    | --------------------------- | -------------------- | ---------------- |
                    | Technology                  | AWS Kinesis          | Apache Kafka     |
                    | Management                  | AWS managed          | AWS managed      |
                    | Streaming                   | ✅                    | ✅                |
                    | Partitions                  | Shards               | Kafka partitions |
                    | Kafka compatibility         | ❌                    | ✅                |
                    | Existing Kafka applications | Need modification    | Usually easier   |
                    | Kafka ecosystem             | ❌                    | ✅                |
                MSK Cluster:
                    When you create an MSK cluster, AWS manages the Kafka broker nodes for you.
                    Kafka Broker:
                        A broker is basically a Kafka server that:
                            receives messages
                            stores messages
                            serves messages to consumers
                    ZooKeeper:
                        Historically, Kafka used Apache ZooKeeper for cluster coordination and metadata management.
                        However, modern Kafka can use KRaft instead of ZooKeeper.
                MSK stores data on EBS to persisted.
                Automatic recovery
                MSK Serverless
                    Apache Kafka without managing cluster capacity.
                MSK Serverless vs regular MSK:
                    |                                  | MSK                                    | MSK Serverless                   |
                    | -------------------------------- | -------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- |
                    | Apache Kafka                     | ✅                                      | ✅                                |
                    | AWS managed                      | ✅                                      | ✅                                |
                    | Manage cluster capacity          | More control                           | ❌                                |
                    | Automatic scaling                | Limited/managed differently            | ✅                                |
                    | Infrastructure capacity planning | More involved                          | Minimal                          |
                    | Best for                         | Predictable/control-oriented workloads | Variable/unpredictable workloads |

                Apache Kafka on AWS → Amazon MSK
                Existing Kafka application → MSK
                Kafka but don't want to manage broker capacity → MSK Serverless
                AWS-native streaming → Kinesis Data Streams
                Deliver streaming data to S3/Redshift/OpenSearch → Kinesis Data Firehose
                Apache Flink processing → Managed Service for Apache Flink
                Streaming ETL with Spark → AWS Glue Streaming ETL
                Kinesis Data Streams VS Amazon MSK:
                    | Feature              | Kinesis Data Streams     | Amazon MSK                         |
                    | -------------------- | ------------------------ | ---------------------------------- |
                    | Technology           | AWS Kinesis              | Apache Kafka                       |
                    | Main container       | Stream                   | Topic                              |
                    | Parallel units       | **Shards**               | **Partitions**                     |
                    | Scaling unit         | Shard                    | Partition / Kafka capacity         |
                    | Change units         | **Split + Merge shards** | **Add partitions**                 |
                    | Message size         | ~1 MB limit              | ~1 MB default, configurable larger |
                    | In-flight encryption | TLS                      | PLAINTEXT or TLS                   |
                    | At-rest encryption   | KMS                      | KMS                                |
                    | Kafka compatibility  | ❌                        | ✅                                  |
                    | Kafka ecosystem      | ❌                        | ✅                                  |
    Big Data Ingestion Pipeline:
                IoT Core
                ↓
                "Collect from IoT devices"

                Kinesis Data Streams
                ↓
                "Real-time streaming"

                Firehose
                ↓
                "Deliver streaming data"

                Lambda
                ↓
                "Lightweight transformation"

                S3
                ↓
                "Store data / Data Lake"

                Athena
                ↓
                "SQL query S3"

                S3
                ↓
                "Store query reports"

                Redshift
                ↓
                "Data Warehouse"

                QuickSight
                ↓
                "Dashboard / BI"
Machine Learning:
    Rekognition:
        to find objects, people, text, scenes in images and videos using ML.
        Face Verification vs Face Search:
            Verification = 1-to-1 comparison
                "Are these two faces the same person?"
            Search = 1-to-many search
                "Which person in my face database is this?"
        Use cases:
            Labeling
            Content Moderation
            Text Detection
            Face Detection and Analysis (gender, age range, emotions...)
            Face Search and Verification
            Celebrity Recognition
            Pathing (ex: for sports game analysis)
            So for example, if you're doing a sports game analysis
        Content Moderation:
            User uploads an image/video → Rekognition checks whether it contains potentially inappropriate content → flag it if necessary.
            uploaded content ကို စစ်ဆေးပြီး မသင့်တော်တဲ့ / offensive / sensitive content တွေကို ရှာဖွေခြင်းပါ။
            Minimum Confidence Threshold:
                ဘယ်လောက်ယုံကြည်မှုရှိမှ content ကို flag လုပ်မလဲဆိုတဲ့ သတ်မှတ်ချက်
                trade-off
                        User
                        │
                        │ Upload photo/video
                        ▼
                        S3
                        │
                        ▼
                        Amazon Rekognition
                        │
                        ├── Safe → Publish
                        │
                        └── Potentially unsafe
                                ↓
                            Flag it
                                ↓
                        Human Review
                Amazon Augmented AI (A2I):
                    Human checks when needed
            Help comply with regulations:
                It doesn't mean:
                    "Rekognition guarantees regulatory compliance."
                    The company is still responsible for its compliance process.
            example:
                    Seller
                    │
                    │ Upload product image
                    ▼
                    S3
                    │
                    ▼
                    Rekognition
                    │
                    ▼
                    Content Moderation
                    │
                    ├── Confidence 99%
                    │       ↓
                    │    Flag automatically
                    │
                    └── Confidence 60%
                            ↓
                        A2I
                            ↓
                        Human reviewer
            Flag = mark something as suspicious / needs attention. 🚩
                eg: Minimum confidence threshold = 80%
                Now Rekognition follows this rule:
                    Confidence >= 80%
                        ↓
                        🚩 FLAG

                    Confidence < 80%
                        ↓
                    Don't flag
    Transcribe:
        convert speech to text
        use a deep learning process called ASR = Automatic Speech Recognition.
        PII Redaction:
            PII = Personally Identifiable Information
        Automatic Language Identification:
            Audio ထဲမှာ ဘာ language ပြောထားလဲဆိုတာကို automatically identify လုပ်နိုင်
        Closed Captioning / Subtitles:
        Searchable Media Archive
        use case:
            customer service
    Polly:
        Text → Speech
        Pronunciation Lexicons:
            a custom pronunciation dictionary: When you see this word, pronounce it this way.
            acronyms:
                can define how Polly should interpret/pronounce a particular written form.
                AWS → "Amazon Web Services"
        SynthesizeSpeech operation:
            SynthesizeSpeech means: Generate speech/audio from text.
        SSML: 
            Speech Synthesis Markup Language
            instructions about how to speak the text, rather than simply giving it plain text.
            Phonetic pronunciation:
    Translate:
        natural and accurate language translation
            Natural = လူပြောသလို သဘာဝကျတဲ့ ဘာသာပြန်မှု
            Accurate = အဓိပ္ပါယ်မှန်ကန်အောင် ဘာသာပြန်ပေးခြင်း
        amazon translate allows you to localize content
            such as websites and applications for international users,
            and to easily tranlate large volumes of text efficiently.
            Localize ဆိုတာ content ကို နိုင်ငံ/ဘာသာစကားအလိုက် ပြောင်းလဲပေးတာ
    Amazon Lex & Connect:
        Amazon Lex = Chatbot / Voice Bot တည်ဆောက်ဖို့ service
            Alexa မှာအသုံးပြုတဲ့ technology နဲ့ ဆက်စပ်တဲ့ conversational AI service
            ASR — Automatic Speech Recognition
            Speech -> Text
            Customer က ပြောလိုက်တာကို Lex က text အဖြစ် နားလည်နိုင်အောင် ပြောင်းပေ
            NLU — Natural Language Understanding
            help build chatbots, call center bots.
        Amazon Connect = Cloud-based Contact Center
            Lex က customer နဲ့ စကားပြောပြီး intent နားလည်တဲ့ bot ဖြစ်တယ်။
            Connect က customer call center တစ်ခုလုံးကို cloud ပေါ်မှာ run ပေးတဲ့ service ဖြစ်တယ်။
            Receive calls
                Customer က company ကို ဖုန်းခေါ်လာတာကို Amazon Connect က လက်ခံပေးနိုင်တယ်။
            Contact Flows
                Contact Flow = customer call ဘယ်လိုအဆင့်ဆင့်သွားမလဲဆိုတာ သတ်မှတ်ထားတဲ့ workflow။
        Note:
            Lex vs Connect:
                |                 | Amazon Lex                 | Amazon Connect                  |
                | --------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------- |
                | အဓိက            | Chat/Voice Bot             | Contact Center                  |
                | အလုပ်           | Customer ရဲ့ intent နားလည် | Calls + contact-center workflow |
                | ASR             | ✅                          | —                               |
                | NLU             | ✅                          | —                               |
                | Phone calls     | Bot အနေနဲ့ handle နိုင်    | ☎️ Call infrastructure          |
                | Contact flows   | —                          | ✅                               |
                | Human agents    | —                          | ✅                               |
                | CRM integration | Indirect/backend           | ✅                               |
    Comprehend:
        Text ကို AI/ML နဲ့ "နားလည်" ပေးတဲ့ NLP service
        text ကို input အဖြစ်ယူပြီး အဲဒီ text ထဲက information တွေကို ရှာပေး
            📝 Text
                ↓
                Amazon Comprehend
                ↓
                "ဒီစာမှာ ဘာတွေပါလဲ?"
                "ဘယ်လို sentiment လဲ?"
                "ဘယ် topic လဲ?"
                "ဘယ်သူ/ဘယ်နေရာ/ဘယ် brand လဲ?"
        Language Detection,
        Key Phrases(အဓိကစကားစုတွေ),
        Entities(နေရာ၊ brand၊ event),
        Sentiment Analysis
            Sentiment = စာရေးသူရဲ့ positive/negative feeling
        Tokenization: စကားလုံး/token အပိုင်းတွေ ခွဲခြမ်းခြင်း
        Parts of Speech: စကားလုံးတစ်ခုချင်းစီရဲ့ grammatical role ကို analyze လုပ်
        Organize Text by Topic:
            Comprehend က text documents အများကြီးကို analyze လုပ်ပြီး ဘယ် topic အကြောင်းလဲ ဆိုတာ automatically ရှာနိုင်တယ်။
            Topic Modelingနဲ့ ဆက်စပ်
        use case:
            Customer Interaction Example
                Company မှာ customer emails 100,000 ရှိတယ်။
                Company က ဒီလို insight ရနိုင်တယ်—
                Negative customer experience အများစုက delivery delay ကြောင့်ဖြစ်နေတယ်။
        Comprehend vs Lex:
            | Service               | Main purpose                 |
            | --------------------- | ---------------------------- |
            | **Amazon Lex**        | Chatbot / conversational bot |
            | **Amazon Comprehend** | Analyze and understand text  |
            example:
                Customer က chatbot ကို—
                    "I want to cancel my order."
                    ပြောတယ်။
                Lex က:
                    "Customer ရဲ့ intent က CancelOrder ဖြစ်တယ်"
                    လို့ နားလည်ပြီး conversation ကို handle လုပ်တယ်။
                Customer က email ရေးတယ်—  "Your delivery was very late and I'm disappointed."
                Comprehend က:
                    Sentiment = Negative
                    Key phrase = delivery
                    Topic = Delivery
                လို analyze လုပ်တယ်။
    Comprehend Medical:
        Medical text ကို နားလည်/analyze
        PHI:
            PHI = Protected Health Information
            ဒါက medical context ထဲက လူနာကို ခွဲခြားသိရှိနိုင်စေတဲ့ ကျန်းမာရေးဆိုင်ရာ အချက်အလက် တွေပါ။
            DetectPHI ရဲ့ key point က PHI ကို detect လုပ်တာ ဖြစ်ပါတယ်။
            ဒီ API ရဲ့ အဓိက concept ကို medical text ထဲက sensitive patient information ရှာဖွေ
        real-time/streaming data use case:
            Real-time medical data
                    ↓
            Kinesis Data Firehose
                    ↓
            Comprehend Medical
                    ↓
            Medical information
    SageMaker AI:
        AWS platform for building, training, deploying, and managing ML models.
                Training Data
                    ↓
                Prepare / Label Data
                    ↓
                Train ML Model
                    ↓
                Evaluate Model
                    ↓
                Deploy Model
                    ↓
                Prediction
    Amazon Kendra:
        Fully managed document search service powered by Machine Learning
            document တွေအများကြီးထဲမှာ user မေးတဲ့အရာကို ရှာပြီး answer ကို document ထဲကနေ ထုတ်ပေးတာ
        Extract answers from within a document.
        Natural Language Search:
            Natural language search ဆိုတာ keyword တွေကို တိတိကျကျ မသိဘဲ လူပြောသလို မေးလို့ရတာ
        Incremental Learning:
            user တွေရဲ့ interaction/feedback ကို အသုံးပြုပြီး search results ကို ပိုကောင်းအောင် learn လုပ်တာပါ။
        Manually Fine-tune Search Results:
            Kendra က automatic learning ပဲမဟုတ်ဘူး။
            Admin ကလည်း—
            "ဒီ result ကို ပိုအရေးကြီးတယ်လို့ သတ်မှတ်ချင်တယ်"
            ဆိုပြီး search results ကို manually influence လုပ်နိုင်တယ်။
        Kendra vs OpenSearch
            OpenSearch: Search engine
                Logs, application data, indexed documents စတာတွေကို fast search/filter လုပ်ဖို့ကောင်း
            Kendra: Intelligent document search
                keyword တစ်လုံးချင်းရှာတာထက် မေးခွန်းရဲ့ meaning ကိုနားလည်ပြီး relevant document/content ထဲက answer ကို ရှာပေးနိုင်
        Kendra vs Comprehend:
            Comprehend:
                "ဒီ text ထဲမှာ ဘာတွေပါလဲ?"
                Customer Email
                    ↓
                Comprehend
                    ↓
                Sentiment = Negative
                Entity = Amazon
                Key phrase = delivery
            Kendra:
                "Documents အများကြီးထဲမှာ ငါမေးတာနဲ့ သက်ဆိုင်တဲ့ answer ဘယ်မှာလဲ?"
                100,000 Documents
                    ↓
                    Kendra
                    ↓
                Question
                    ↓
                Relevant answer
    Personalize:
        Recommendation
        User တစ်ယောက်
        ချင်းစီအတွက် ဘာကို recommend လုပ်သင့်လဲဆိုတာ ML နဲ့ ခန့်မှန်းပေးတဲ့ managed service
        same website ဖြစ်ပေမယ့် user တစ်ယောက်ချင်းစီကို recommendation မတူအောင် လုပ်ပေးနိုင်
        keywords:
            Personalized recommendations
            Product recommendations
            Real-time recommendations
            Recommendation engine
            Personalized content
            Re-ranking
            User behavior/preferences
            Customized marketing
            Retail recommendations
            Media recommendations
            "Customers who bought this also..."
        Re-ranking:
            ရှိပြီးသား item list ကို user အတွက် ပိုသင့်တော်တဲ့ order နဲ့ ပြန်စီပေးခြင်း
        don't need to build, train, deploy ML
    Textract:
        Extract text and structured data from documents using ML/OCR.
            OCR = Optical Character Recognition
            "Look at this image and recognize the characters."
        can understand the structure of documents.
        can understand the table structure.
        can also detect selection elements such as checkboxes.
        Key-Value Pair extraction.
        keywords:
            OCR
            Extract text from documents
            Extract data from scanned documents
            Forms
            Key-value pairs
            Tables
            Checkboxes / selection elements
            Invoices
            Receipts
            PDF documents
            Scanned documents
            Automatically extract structured data from documents
        if the question emphasizes documents, forms, tables, invoices, PDFs, think Textract.

AWS Monitoring & Audit:
    CloudWatch:
        Overview:
            Log Group:
                basically a container/category for related logs.
                Log Stream:
                    Inside a Log Group, you have Log Streams.
                    Log Group
                        └── Web Application
                            │
                            ├── Log Stream → EC2-01
                            ├── Log Stream → EC2-02
                            └── Log Stream → EC2-03
                    Log Events:
                        an individual log entry.
                            Log Group
                            ↓
                            Log Stream
                            ↓
                            Log Events
            Log Retention / Expiration:
                Can define log expiration policies (never expire, 1 day to 10 years...)
                    Retention = 30 days
                Because keeping logs forever costs money.
            Arbitrary name:
                AWS doesn't force one particular naming convention. You choose the name.
            CloudWatch Logs → Other AWS Services:
                CloudWatch Logs doesn't have to be the final destination.
                CloudWatch Logs
                    │
                    ├──→ S3
                    ├──→ Kinesis Data Streams
                    ├──→ Kinesis Data Firehose
                    ├──→ Lambda
                    └──→ OpenSearch
            Encryption:
                Logs are encrypted by default.
                supports encryption at rest.
                use your own KMS key.
            Note:
                Group = application
                Stream = source
                Event = actual log entry
        CloudWatch Logs Insights:
            CloudWatch Logs ထဲက log data ကို SQL-like query နဲ့ search/analyze လုပ်တဲ့ query engine
            CloudWatch Logs Insights => Stored logs ကို search/analyze
            use case:
                Count ERROR:
                    Logs Insights က—
                        Search
                        ↓
                        Filter ERROR
                        ↓
                        Count
                        ↓
                        ERROR = 3
                    လို aggregate calculation လုပ်နိုင်
                    Aggregate statistics:
                        data အများကြီးကို စုစည်းပြီး count, average, sum စတဲ့ summary result ထုတ်တာ
            Query Language:
                Logs Insights မှာ SQL မဟုတ်တဲ့ purpose-built query language
                CloudWatch Logs အတွက် အထူးတည်ဆောက်ထားတဲ့ query language နဲ့ query လုပ်တယ်
            Automatically discovers fields:
                application က JSON log ထုတ်တယ်
                Logs Insights က field တွေကို သိရှိနိုင်တယ်
            Save Queries → Dashboard
                Query ကို save လုပ်ထားပြီး CloudWatch Dashboard မှာထည့်နိုင်
                ဒါဆို dashboard ကနေ application health ကို လွယ်လွယ်ကြည့်နိုင်
            Multiple Log Groups / AWS Accounts:
                Logs Insights က supported setup နဲ့ multiple Log Groups / different AWS accounts ကို query လုပ်နိုင်
            It's a query engine, not a real-time engine"
                ရှိပြီးသား CloudWatch Logs ကို query/search/analyze လုပ်တာ
                real-time streaming processing engine မဟုတ်ပါဘူး။
                ဥပမာ—
                    "Log event အသစ်ဝင်လာတာနဲ့ ချက်ချင်း process လုပ်ချင်တယ်"
                    အဲဒီလို requirement ဆိုရင် Kinesis / Lambda / OpenSearch စတဲ့ architecture ကို စဉ်းစားရမယ်
            ExportTask → S3 = batch / delayed
                CreateExportTask can use. but,
                    Log data can take up to 12 hours to become available for export.
                    So this is not a real-time solution.
                    eg: for yesterday's logs => perfect, use CreateExportTask
                    eg: for Every new error log => Don't use CreateExportTask.
            Subscription → Kinesis/Lambda = real-time
                If you need real-time log events, use a Logs Subscription.
                    Application
                        ↓
                    CloudWatch Logs
                        ↓
                    Subscription
                        ↓
                    ┌─────────────┬──────────────┬─────────┐
                    ↓             ↓              ↓
                    Kinesis       Firehose       Lambda
                    Data Streams
                Subscription Filter:
                        Suppose CloudWatch has millions of logs:
                            INFO
                            INFO
                            INFO
                            ERROR
                            INFO
                        But you only care about: ERROR
                        You can use a Subscription Filter.
                ExportTask vs Subscription
                    |                         | ExportTask           | Logs Subscription           |
                    | ----------------------- | -------------------- | --------------------------- |
                    | Main purpose            | Export/archive logs  | Real-time processing        |
                    | Destination             | **S3**               | Kinesis / Firehose / Lambda |
                    | Timing                  | Delayed/batch        | **Real-time**               |
                    | Filter specific events? | Not the main concept | **Subscription Filter**     |
                    | Common use              | Long-term archive    | Processing / analysis       |
                Cross-account subscription:
                    Account A ရဲ့ CloudWatch Logs ကို Account B ကနေ real-time လက်ခံပြီး process/analyze လုပ်ခြင်း။
                CloudWatch Logs for EC2
                    EC2 → CloudWatch Logs does NOT happen automatically.
                    By default:
                        EC2
                        ├── /var/log/app.log
                        ├── /var/log/messages
                        └── /var/log/secure
                    These are just files inside the EC2 instance.
                    You need:
                        EC2
                        ↓
                        CloudWatch Agent
                        ↓
                        CloudWatch Logs
                    CloudWatch Agent:
                        a small program running inside your EC2 instance.
                        Its job is:
                            Watch these log files and send their contents to CloudWatch.
                            needs permission to call CloudWatch APIs and send logs.
                            Usually, you attach an IAM role to the EC2 instance with the necessary CloudWatch permissions.
                        Example: Web server
                            Imagine you have an EC2 running Nginx.
                            Nginx creates:
                                /var/log/nginx/access.log
                                /var/log/nginx/error.log
                            By default:
                                EC2
                                │
                                ├── access.log
                                └── error.log
                                ❌ Nothing automatically goes to CloudWatch

                                Install/configure CloudWatch Agent:
                                EC2
                                │
                                ├── access.log ──┐
                                │                │
                                └── error.log ───┤
                                                ↓
                                        CloudWatch Agent
                                                ↓
                                        CloudWatch Logs
                                                ↓
                                        Log Group / Streams
                                Now you can use CloudWatch Logs Insights to query them.
                                    EC2 → CloudWatch Agent → CloudWatch Logs → Logs Insights
                        Agent can collect more than logs:
                            ├── Log files
                        ├── Memory utilization
                        ├── Disk utilization
                        └── Other system metrics
                    CloudWatch Logs Agent & Unified Agent:
                        Logs Agent = logs only
                            "Take log files and send them to CloudWatch Logs."
                        Unified Agent = logs + system metrics
                            can send: System-level metrics
                    Logs vs Metrics:
                        Logs(old CloudWatch Agent)
                            Detailed records/events:
                                10:01 User logged in
                                10:02 Order created
                                10:03 ERROR database timeout

                                → CloudWatch Logs
                        Metrics(new CloudWatch Agent)
                            Numerical measurements over time:
                                10:01 → RAM 60%
                                10:02 → RAM 65%
                                10:03 → RAM 82%

                                → CloudWatch Metrics
                        The Unified Agent can collect both.
                SSM Parameter Store:
                    Centralized configuration using SSM Parameter Store
                    This is useful when you have many servers.
                    You don't want to manually configure every agent separately.
                    Instead, you can store the agent configuration centrally in:
                    AWS Systems Manager Parameter Store
        CloudWatch Alarm:
            Watch a metric and take an action when a condition is met.
            Alarm States:
                OK: metric is within the expected condition.
                    Example:
                        CPU = 40%
                        Threshold = 80%
                ALARM: threshold condition has been met/breached.
                    Example:
                        CPU = 90%
                        Threshold = 80%
                INSUFFICIENT_DATA: 
                    CloudWatch doesn't have enough data to determine whether the alarm should be OK or ALARM.
                    Example:
                        Alarm:
                        CPU > 80%

                        But CloudWatch receives: (no data)

                        CloudWatch can't confidently say:
                            CPU > 80% ? 🤔
                        So:
                        → INSUFFICIENT_DATA
            Threshold:
                value that determines when the alarm condition is triggered.
            Period:
                how long CloudWatch evaluates the metric data for each evaluation.
                Example:
                    Period = 60 seconds
                    means CloudWatch evaluates data in 1-minute intervals.
                    10:00 ───────── 10:01
                            1 minute
            High-resolution custom metrics:
                10 sec, 30 sec or multiples of 60 sec
                This is useful when you need faster monitoring.
            Alarm doesn't necessarily mean "send email":
                common architecture:
                    EC2 CPU
                    ↓
                    CloudWatch Metric
                    ↓
                    CloudWatch Alarm
                    ↓
                    SNS
                    ↓
                    Email
                Or:
                    EC2 CPU
                    ↓
                    CloudWatch Alarm
                    ↓
                    Auto Scaling
                    ↓
                    Add EC2 instance
            Alarm Targets:
                Alarm = detects a problem
                Alarm Target/Action = what happens after the problem is detected.
                EC2 Actions:
                    CloudWatch Alarm can perform actions on an EC2 instance:
                    Alarm
                        ↓
                        EC2
                        ├── Stop
                        ├── Terminate
                        ├── Reboot
                        └── Recover
                    Stop:
                        If CPU stays at 5% for a long time, stop an unnecessary EC2.
                            CPU very low
                                    ↓
                                Alarm
                                    ↓
                                STOP EC2
                    Terminate:
                        Alarm ka EC2 instance ကို ဖျက်ပစ် လိုက်တယ်။
                        Production EC2 မှာ သတိထားသုံးရမယ့် action
                    Reboot:
                        EC2 ကို restart လုပ်တယ်။
                        ဥပမာ server တစ်ခုမှာ problem ဖြစ်ပြီး reboot လုပ်ရင် ပြန်ကောင်းနိုင်တဲ့ situation။
                    Recover:
                        EC2 underlying hardware problem ဖြစ်တဲ့အခါ instance recovery လုပ်နိုင်ပါတယ်။
                        EC2 Instance Recovery:
                            Status Check = EC2 ရဲ့ ဘယ်အပိုင်းမှာ ပြဿနာဖြစ်နေလဲ စစ်တာ
                            System Status Check failure → underlying AWS hardware/infrastructure ပြဿနာ → Recover လုပ်နိုင်
                            EC2 Status Checks:
                                mainly 3 types:
                                    Instance Status = VM
                                    System Status = AWS hardware
                                    EBS Status = Storage
                                Instance Status Check:
                                    EC2 VM / instance အတွင်းပိုင်းကို စစ်တာ
                                System Status Check:
                                    EC2 instance အောက်က physical/underlying AWS infrastructure ကို စစ်တာ
                                Attached EBS Status:
                                    EC2 နဲ့ attach လုပ်ထားတဲ့ EBS volume တွေကို စစ်တာ
                                If recovery, 
                                    Recovery လုပ်ပြီးတဲ့အခါ instance ရဲ့ configuration/network identity အများစုကို ဆက်ထိန်းထားပါတယ်။
                                    Same Private, public, elastic IP, metadata, params, etc.
                Trigger Auto Scaling Action
                Send notification to SNS
            Composite Alarm:
                အခြား CloudWatch Alarms တွေရဲ့ STATE ကို monitor လုပ်တယ်
            Normal Alarm vs Composite Alarm
                Normal Alarm
                    CPU Metric
                        ↓
                    CloudWatch Alarm
                    ဥပမာ: CPU > 80% → ALARM
                Composite Alarm
                    Alarm A ──┐
                            ├──→ Composite Alarm
                    Alarm B ──┘
                Composite Alarm က CPU metric ကို တိုက်ရိုက်ကြည့်တာမဟုတ်ဘူး
                AND/OR condition:
                    Alarm A AND Alarm B => do something.
            Why Composite Alarms? → Reduce Alarm Noise
                Alarm noise ဆိုတာ—
                    Problem မဟုတ်သေးတဲ့ minor alerts တွေကြောင့် notification အများကြီးတက်လာပြီး
                    administrator ကို alert fatigue ဖြစ်စေတာ။
                    CPU တစ်ခုတည်း ခဏတက်သွားတာနဲ့ alert ပို့နေရင်—
                    လူက alert တွေ အရမ်းများလာလို့ တကယ့် critical problem ကို မမြင်နိုင်တော့ဘူး။
                        Alarm 1: CPU High
                        AND
                        Alarm 2: Memory High
                        AND
                        Alarm 3: Application Error High
                            ↓
                        Composite Alarm
                            ↓
                        🚨 Critical Problem
                            ↓
                        SNS
                            ↓
                        Administrator
                        ဒီလို တကယ် serious ဖြစ်တဲ့အချိန်မှာပဲ notification ပို့နိုင်တယ်။
            CloudWatch Logs → Metric Filter → Alarm:
                CloudWatch Logs ထဲက log message ကို Metric Filter နဲ့ metric အဖြစ်ပြောင်းပြီး Alarm တပ်နိုင်ပါတယ်
                    Application
                        ↓
                    CloudWatch Logs
                        ↓
                    Metric Filter
                    "ERROR" ပါတဲ့ log ကိုရှာ
                        ↓
                    Custom CloudWatch Metric
                    ErrorCount = 10
                        ↓
                    CloudWatch Alarm
                    ErrorCount > 5
                        ↓
                    SNS → Email
            Alarm ကို test လုပ်ခြင်း:
                ဒီ command က တကယ် metric threshold ကျော်သွားအောင်လုပ်တာ မဟုတ်ပါဘူး။
                    eg:  aws cloudwatch set-alarm-state --alarm-name "CPU > 95%" --state-value ALARM --state-reason "Testing alarm actions"
                SNS email notification ရောက်မရောက်, Alarm action အလုပ်လုပ်မလုပ် စမ်းနိုင်
            Note:
                Normal Alarm
                    ↓
                Metric တစ်ခုကိုကြည့်

                Composite Alarm
                    ↓
                Alarm တွေရဲ့ STATE ကိုကြည့်
                    ↓
                AND / OR
        CloudWatch Network Synthetic Monitor:
            AWS ↔ On-premises network connection ကို CloudWatch နဲ့ စောင့်ကြည့်ပေးတဲ့ synthetic network test
            AWS ထဲမှာရှိတဲ့ application/server က on-premises server ကို ဆက်သွယ်တဲ့ network path မှာ ပြဿနာရှိမရှိ စစ်ပေးပါတယ်။
                ဥပမာ
                Latency ↑ → connection နှေးလာ
                Packet loss ↑ → packet တချို့ပျောက်
                Jitter ↑ → latency က တစ်ခါနဲ့တစ်ခါ မတည်ငြိမ်
            "Synthetic" ဆိုတာဘာလဲ?
                Real user traffic ကို စောင့်ကြည့်တာမဟုတ်ဘဲ monitoring အတွက် test traffic ကို ကိုယ်တိုင် generate လုပ်ပြီး စမ်းသပ်တာပါ။
                    Network Synthetic Monitor
                            │
                            ├── ICMP test
                            │
                            └── TCP test
                                │
                                ▼
                        On-premises IP
                ဥပမာ—
                AWS ကနေ On-prem server 10.0.0.10 ကို TCP connection test လုပ်ပြီး latency/packet loss စစ်မယ်။
                ဒါကြောင့် Synthetic = simulated/test traffic လို့ မှတ်ပါ။
            Agent မလိုဘူး
                On-prem server မှာ monitoring agent install လုပ်စရာ မလိုပါဘူး။
            can check route of :
                AWS Direct Connect
                Site-to-Site VPN
            Traffic protocol အနေနဲ့—
                ICMP
                TCP
            CloudWatch Metrics ထဲရောက်တယ်
                Monitor လုပ်တဲ့ result တွေကို CloudWatch Metrics အဖြစ် publish လုပ်ပါတယ်။
                        AWS / On-Prem Network
                                ↓
                        Network Synthetic Monitor
                                ↓
                        Latency
                        Packet Loss
                        Jitter
                                ↓
                        CloudWatch Metrics
                                ↓
                        CloudWatch Alarm
                                ↓
                        SNS / Notification
        Amazon EventBridge:
            a serverless event bus that lets AWS services and your applications react to events.
            Schedule — "Do something at a specific time"
                TIME-based trigger similar to a cron job.
            Event Pattern — "Something happened"
                Event listener
            EventBridge trigger:
                doesn't necessarily do the actual work itself.
                routes the event to a Target:
                    Lambda
                    SQS
                    SNS
                    Step Functions
                    ECS tasks
                    API destinations
                    other EventBridge buses
            EventBridge Rule:
                can be based on either:
                    Schedule
                    OR
                    Event Pattern
            EventBridge vs CloudWatch Alarm:
                CloudWatch Alarm : Metric crossed a threshold → take action
                EventBridge: An event happened → route/react
            Event Bus + Resource-based Policy:
                Event Bus is where events are received and routed.
                By default, an event bus is mainly used within its AWS account. 
                But you can allow other AWS accounts to send events to your event bus using a resource-based policy.
                        Account A                       Account B
                        Application
                            │
                            │ Event
                            ▼
                        EventBridge ────────────────────► Event Bus
                                                        │
                                                        ▼
                                                    Rule
                                                        │
                                                        ▼
                                                    Lambda
                    PutEvents:
                        Send/publish an event to an EventBridge event bus.
                EventBridge Archive:
                    can archive events that arrive at an Event Bus.
                    You can choose:
                        Archive all events
                        Archive only events matching a filter/event pattern
                        Keep them for a configured retention period
                EventBridge Replay:
                    Archive = save the past events
                    Replay = send those saved events through the Event Bus again
                        example:
                            Imagine you deployed a buggy Lambda:
                                S3 Event
                                ↓
